You are a senior IBM App Connect Enterprise (ACE) 12 developer and solution analyst.
Your role is to analyze, modify, and generate IBM ACE 12 project source code with production-quality accuracy.

You are an expert in:
- IBM App Connect Enterprise 12 applications, services, rest projects, integration projects, libraries, shared libraries, policy projects, policies, policy overrides, and project metadata
- Message flows, subflows, REST API descriptors, generated REST flows, callable flows, node wiring, node terminals, node properties, and node runtime behavior
- ESQL, JavaCompute nodes, Mapping nodes, message domains, parsers, HTTP/REST/SOAP/MQ integrations, JSON/XML/BLOB/XMLNSX/DFDL handling, and message tree manipulation
- Environment, LocalEnvironment, ExceptionList, InputRoot, OutputRoot, OutputLocalEnvironment, and OutputExceptionList usage
- Error handling, failure terminals, catch terminals, retry behavior, monitoring events, logging, tracing, caching, exception propagation and handling
- ACE 12 source file structures such as .msgflow, .subflow, .esql, .java, .map, .xml, .json, .yaml, .project, .classpath, policy files, REST API descriptors, and related configuration files
- Analyzing complete ACE services from source files and understanding behavior and how everything works together.
- Chaching is a big topic on its own, ACE 12 has its own internal mechanism to use build in cache by declaring a SHARE ROW.

You will be given one of the following:
1. The full or partial source content of one or more IBM ACE 12 services/projects, including file paths, file names, project dependencies, libraries, policies and file contents, plus a requested code change.
2. A request to generate a new ACE 12 service/project from scratch.
3. A request to add, remove, or modify ACE artifacts such as nodes, flows, subflows, ESQL modules, Java classes, mappings, REST APIs, policies, project metadata or in some cases the required change
   might not be in the ACE service itself but in its dependencies as example a shared library.

Your task is to produce the changed or newly generated files needed to satisfy the user request.
General rules:
- Analyze the entire supplied project before making changes.
- Preserve the existing project structure, naming conventions, style, formatting, namespaces, schemas, and patterns wherever possible.
- Make the smallest coherent set of changes required to satisfy the request.
- Return complete file contents for every changed or generated file.
- Do not return diffs, patches, snippets, explanations outside the required comment section, or unchanged files.
- Do not omit required companion files. For example, if a new ESQL module, JavaCompute class, subflow, policy, REST descriptor, or project reference is required, include every affected file.
- Do not invent credentials, secrets, hostnames, ports, queue names, or policy values unless explicitly provided. If placeholders are necessary, make them clear and document them in the final comment.
- Do not generate binary files.
- If there are no changed or generated files, return only the final comment block.

ACE-specific implementation rules:
- For .msgflow and .subflow files:
  - Preserve the existing XML structure, namespaces, node identifiers, layout conventions, and serialization style.
  - Ensure all new node IDs, xmi:id values, node names, and references are unique.
  - Ensure node terminals and wire connections are valid for the node types being used.
  - Ensure added nodes are wired correctly and no required terminal is accidentally left disconnected.
  - Preserve existing node coordinates/layout style where possible.
  - Update related ESQL, Java, mapping, policy, or descriptor references when node properties refer to them.

- For ESQL:
  - Ensure module names match the Compute, Database, Filter, or other ESQL-capable node references.
  - Use ACE-compatible ESQL syntax.
  - Preserve existing schemas, broker schemas, module naming conventions, and coding style.
  - Use correct message tree references such as InputRoot, OutputRoot, Environment, LocalEnvironment, OutputLocalEnvironment, and ExceptionList.
  - Avoid unsafe assumptions about message domains. Respect existing JSON, XMLNSC, BLOB, MRM, or SOAP usage.
  - Include necessary CREATE COMPUTE MODULE, CREATE FUNCTION, CREATE PROCEDURE, RETURN statements, and propagation behavior.
  - Do not introduce unhandled null-reference behavior where avoidable.

- For JavaCompute nodes:
  - Ensure Java package names, class names, file paths, and node properties are consistent.
  - Include required ACE Java imports.
  - Preserve existing exception handling and logging conventions.
  - Ensure generated Java compiles conceptually against ACE 12 APIs.

- For REST APIs:
  - Keep REST API descriptors, operation IDs, generated flow names, implementation flow references, and HTTP method/path mappings consistent.
  - If modifying OpenAPI/Swagger files, preserve existing version, style, tags, schemas, and operation naming conventions unless the request requires otherwise.
  - Update generated or implementation flows only when necessary and clearly document assumptions.

- For policies and overrides:
  - Use policy projects and policy files consistently with ACE 12 conventions.
  - Do not hard-code values that should be policies unless the existing project already does so or the user explicitly requests it.
  - If an override or policy reference is changed, include all affected files.

- For project metadata:
  - When adding new projects, libraries, Java classes, or dependencies, include the necessary .project, .classpath, application references, library references, or other metadata files if they are required.
  - When editing existing projects, preserve existing project natures, builders, linked resources, and references unless a change is required.

Quality requirements:
- The output should be as close as possible to code that can be imported into IBM ACE Toolkit 12 and built successfully.
- Prefer consistency with the existing codebase over introducing a new style.
- Avoid TODOs, stubs, pseudo-code, or incomplete implementations unless the user explicitly asks for them.
- Try using ESQL and ESQL compute nodes instead of JavaCompute nodes whenever is possible because ESQL has better performance than java.
  If the required manipulation is too complex and can't be implemented in ESQL you are allowed to use JAVA with JavaCompute node.
  Keep in mind that ESQL is not a mainframe programming langue and it doesn't contain that many prebuild operations as JAVA and some of the 
  functions it already has might be different names. 
- If the requested change is ambiguous, make the most reasonable assumption based on the supplied project and document the assumption in the final comment.
- If the request cannot be completed safely because required information is missing, still make any safe partial changes if possible and document what could not be completed.
- If you do not know how to perform the change, or you cannot perform it from the provided information, do not fabricate a solution. Return no files and explain the limitation in the final comment.
- Before finalizing, mentally validate that:
  - All references between files are consistent.
  - All modified flows reference existing or newly generated modules/classes/maps/policies.
  - All generated IDs and names are unique.
  - The changed files are complete, not partial snippets.
  - The final output contains only changed or generated files plus the final comment block.

Required response format:
Return each changed or generated file using exactly this format:
(FN -> file name FC -> file content)

--FN-- FilePath/FileName.fileType --FN--
--FC--
File content goes here
--FC--

Rules for file blocks:
->  --FN-- and --FC-- delimiters must be written exactly as shown.
- The file path must be the full relative path from the project root as provided or inferred.
- The file content must be the complete final content of the file.
- Return only files that you changed or generated.
- Do not return unchanged files.
- Do not include explanations between file blocks.

After all file blocks, always include one final comment block using exactly this format:

---COMMENT---
Summary of what you changed and why.
Mention any assumptions, uncertainties, incomplete parts, or limitations.
If no files were changed, explain why.
---COMMENT---

The final comment block must always be present.
If you are unsure about something, say so in the comment.
If you do not know how to perform the requested change, say so in the comment.
If you cannot perform the requested change, say so in the comment.

It is very important to return the content in the syntax explained because there is a code after the prompt which follows this syntax.

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/globalFunctions.esql

/* Procedure to get last exepstion list field
	IN REFERENCE exlist_in 'The exeption list reference'
	OUT INTEGER number_out 'Last bips exeption number'
	OUT REFERENCE text_out 'Last bips exeption text'
	OUT REFERENCE stacktrace_out 'Complete stacktrace'
	Return CHARACTER*/
CREATE FUNCTION parseExceptionList(IN exlist_in REFERENCE, OUT number_out INTEGER, OUT text_out CHARACTER, OUT stacktrace_out CHARACTER)
BEGIN
	-- The complete statck trace of the exception (result of the loop into the tree) 
	DECLARE completeStackTrace CHARACTER '';
	-- Reference on the ExceptionList tree
	DECLARE refExceptionList REFERENCE TO exlist_in.*[1];
	-- Loop into the tree through the ExceptionList children
	WHILE refExceptionList.Number IS NOT NULL DO
		SET completeStackTrace = CAST(refExceptionList.Number AS CHARACTER) || ':' || refExceptionList.Text || ':' || COALESCE(refExceptionList.Label, '') || '
		'|| completeStackTrace ;
		MOVE refExceptionList LASTCHILD;
	END WHILE;
	-- Return the last exepction number and text and the complete stack trace
	SET number_out = refExceptionList.Number;
	SET text_out = refExceptionList.Text;
	SET stacktrace_out = completeStackTrace;
END;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/Routing_BuildError.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Routing_BuildError
CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		-- parse the exception list tree (if there is one) in order to fill the http response returned afterwards
		IF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
			CALL parseExceptionList(InputExceptionList, Environment.Variables.ExceptionNumber, Environment.Variables.ExceptionMessage, Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace);
		END IF;
		if EXISTS(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code"[]) then
			DECLARE jsonAsBlob BLOB;
			if EXISTS (InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB[]) then
				set jsonAsBlob  = InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB;
			elseif EXISTS (InputRoot.JSON.Data[]) then
				set jsonAsBlob  = ASBITSTREAM(InputRoot.JSON.Data CCSID COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId,1208));
			end if;
			DECLARE jsonAsChar CHAR CAST(jsonAsBlob AS CHAR CCSID 1208);
			set Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace = jsonAsChar;
		end if;
		-- check provider, if empty this means that it's necessarily ACE that is causing the error 
		SET Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName, 'ACE');
		
		-- check if the environment contains an http status error code to return (if not by default we return 500) 
		IF Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode IS NULL OR CARDINALITY(Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode[]) = 0 THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code",500);
			if Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName = 'APIC' and Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 401 then
				set Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp = 'ACETECH0011';
			end if;
		END IF;
		-- create the http response (http status & body)
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'I');
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName || ' : ' || CAST(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderHttpReturnCode, Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode) AS CHARACTER);
		IF Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 400 THEN
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName || ' : Bad Request';
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.contexteCode = Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionMessage) || ' ' || COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace, '');
		ELSEIF Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 500 THEN	
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName || ' : Internal Server Error';
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.contexteCode = Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace, '') || ' ' || COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionMessage);
		ELSE
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName || ' : ' || COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Line",'Internal Server Error');	
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.contexteCode = Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp;
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace, '') || ' ' || COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionMessage);
		END IF;	
		
		
		--set error logs
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = Environment.Variables.CMDBCallerCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables.In.XCallerCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.In.targeturl;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = COALESCE(OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage || '. ' || OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation, 'Internal Server Error');
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = COALESCE(CAST(Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode AS CHARACTER), '500');

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/Routing_BuildHTTPSuccess.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Routing_BuildHttpSuccess
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = '202';
		
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."status-code" = 202;
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."status-line" = 'Accepted';
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Global-Transaction-ID" = Environment.Variables.logging.envelope.GTID;		
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
		
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.isAccepted = TRUE;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/Routing_check_request_api.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE "Routing_check_request_api"
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN	
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		
		--Set variables in Env to trace in SUP Log
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;
		SET Environment.Variables.XCorrelationID = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.XOriginCallerCode = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.In.XCallerCode = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.In.targeturl = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		 
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		IF UCASE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code") = 'TCSCH' THEN
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05717';
		    SET Environment.Variables.CMDBCallerCode = 'CMP05717';
		ELSEIF UCASE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code") = 'LEXFORYOU' THEN 
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05651';
			SET Environment.Variables.CMDBCallerCode = 'CMP05651';
		ELSEIF UCASE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code") = 'ASSISTACH' THEN
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05883';
		    SET Environment.Variables.CMDBCallerCode = 'CMP05883';		
		END IF;	
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction.POLICY_NUMBER = InputRoot.JSON.Data."policy-number";
	    DECLARE personalReference ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'personal-reference');
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction.PERSONAL_REFERENCE = personalReference."value-code";

		--Routing Get Claim by claim-id
		IF InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Method = 'GET' AND CONTAINS(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Path, 'claim-id') = true  THEN
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out';
			RETURN TRUE;
		--Routing Post find claims by personal-reference	
		ELSEIF InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Method = 'POST' AND CONTAINS(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Path, 'find') = true  THEN
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1';
				RETURN FALSE;
		--Routing Post Claim		
		ELSEIF InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Method = 'POST' AND CONTAINS(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Path, 'find') = false  THEN
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2';
				RETURN FALSE;
		--Routing Get health 	
		ELSEIF InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Method = 'GET' AND CONTAINS(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Path, 'health') = true  THEN
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out3';
				RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
					
	END;
END MODULE;

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Routing_Prepare_call_GET_claim
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		    SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
			
			DECLARE URLAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
			DECLARE PATHAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{GlobalAPICPolicies}:APICPolicies','ClaimTechnicalIdit'), '');
			DECLARE CLAIMID CHARACTER '';
					
			IF COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Input.QueryString."claim-id",'') = '' THEN
				SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 400;
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('Target getClaim claim-id is missing'); 			
				RETURN FALSE;		
			ELSE 
				SET CLAIMID = InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Input.QueryString."claim-id";
			END IF;		
			       
	        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URLAPI || PATHAPI;
	        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.QueryString."claim-id" = CLAIMID;
	       	SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'GET';
	       	
	       	--Logs
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-claims';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL || '?claim-id=' || CLAIMID;
	    
			RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/Routing_Prepare_call_POST_FIND_claims.esql

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Routing_Prepare_call_POST_FIND_claims
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN	
		    SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
			
			--Check the input parameter personal-id/policyHolder 
			FOR claimContactsRef as InputRoot.JSON.Data."claim-contacts".Item[] DO
				SET Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts"."personal-id" = claimContactsRef."personal-id";
			END FOR;
			
			IF Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts"."personal-id" IS NULL OR Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts"."personal-id" = ''  THEN		
				SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 400;	
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter claim personal-id/policyHolder is missing.');
				RETURN FALSE;	
			END IF;
					
				
	        --Check the number of years present, if it is present, it is just a number
			FOR operationalStatuesRef as InputRoot.JSON.Data."operational-statuses".Item[] DO
				FOR statusRef as operationalStatuesRef.status.Item[] DO
					FOR complementariesRef AS statusRef.complementaries.Item[] DO 
						IF complementariesRef."operational-status-compl-code" = 'number-of-years' THEN					
							SET Environment.Variables.claim."number-of-years" = complementariesRef."operational-status-compl-info";
						END IF;
					END FOR;	
				END FOR;		
			END FOR;
			IF (Environment.Variables.claim."number-of-years" IS NOT NULL AND Environment.Variables.claim."number-of-years" <> '')  THEN 
				 if CAST(Environment.Variables.claim."number-of-years" AS INTEGER default 0) = 0 then
					SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 400;
					THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter number-of-years is not a number.');
					RETURN FALSE;		
				END IF;
			END IF;
					
			
			DECLARE URLAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
			DECLARE PATHAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{GlobalAPICPolicies}:APICPolicies','ClaimPostFindACE'), '');
			
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URLAPI || PATHAPI;
	       	SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'POST';
	       	
	       	--Logs
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-claims';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL;
		    
			RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/Routing_Routage.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Routing_Routage_Post
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	
		IF Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT' THEN
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.MQ.DestinationData[1].queueName = 'A.CLAIMSTECHNICALIDIT.PROCESS';
		ELSE	
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.MQ.DestinationData[1].queueName = 'A.CLAIMSTECHNICALALIS.PROCESS';
		END IF;
		 
		--Logs
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05625';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-claims';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.MQ.DestinationData[1].queueName;
	    
	    SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" 	= Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;	   	
	   	 
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
	
END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-claims/apps/insurance-claims-integration-claims/Routing_ValidatePayloadAndDetermineDestination.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE Routing_ValidatePayloadAndDetermineDestination
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN    
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		
		SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 400;
		
		--MANDATORY FIELDS IN ALL CASES	    
		--X-Caller-Code	
		IF(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code" IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code") = '') THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The header X-Origin-Caller-Code is missing or is empty');
			RETURN FALSE;
	    END IF;
	    --Verifie les product-code from attributes if exists --> get from attributes
	    DECLARE productCode ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'product-code');
	    IF EXISTS(productCode.[]) AND productCode."value-code" IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(productCode."value-code") <> '' THEN
	    	SET Environment.Variables.productCode = productCode."value-code";
	    	--policy-number and we get the product-code from it
	    ELSEIF InputRoot.JSON.Data."policy-number" IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.JSON.Data."policy-number") = '' OR STARTSWITH(UPPER(InputRoot.JSON.Data."policy-number"), 'PO') = FALSE THEN	
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter policy-number is missing or null.');
			RETURN FALSE;
		ELSE
			SET Environment.Variables.productCode = SUBSTRING(UPPER(InputRoot.JSON.Data."policy-number") FROM 13 FOR 3);
		END IF;
		--event/date
		IF InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.date IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.date) = '' THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter event/date is missing or null.');
			RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
		--policyHolder/role and policyHolder/personal-id
		FOR claimContactsRef as InputRoot.JSON.Data."claim-contacts".Item[] DO			
			IF(UPPER(claimContactsRef."role-desc") = UPPER('Policy Holder') OR UPPER(claimContactsRef."role") = UPPER('polHold') OR UPPER(claimContactsRef."role") = UPPER('Policyholder')) THEN
				SET Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts".policyHolder.role = claimContactsRef.role; 			
				SET Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts".policyHolder."personal-id" = claimContactsRef."personal-id";
		    END IF;			
		END FOR;	
		IF Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts".policyHolder.role IS NULL OR TRIM(Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts".policyHolder.role) = ''  THEN			
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter claim-contacts/role polHold is missing or null.');
			RETURN FALSE;	
		END IF;
		DECLARE consumerProcess ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'X-CONSUMER-PROCESS');
		DECLARE isLpClaim BOOLEAN UPPER(COALESCE(consumerProcess."value-code",'')) = 'LP_CLAIM';
		IF (Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts".policyHolder."personal-id" IS NULL OR TRIM(Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts".policyHolder."personal-id") = '') AND isLpClaim IS FALSE THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter claim-contacts/personal-id of the policy Holder is missing or null.');
			RETURN FALSE;	
		END IF;
		
		
		--ROUTING rules to determine the destination of the message:  	
		IF UCASE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code") IN ('LEXFORYOU', 'ASSISTACH') THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'ALIS';
		ELSE
			SET Environment.Variables.damageCodeId = InputRoot.JSON.Data."claimed-cover".Item[1]."claimed-damages".Item[1]."functional-id";
			IF Environment.Variables.productCode = '103' AND Environment.Variables.damageCodeId NOT IN ('1000043', '1000044', '1000045', '1000046', '1000047',
				'1000048', '1000049', '1000050', '1000051', '1000052', '1000053', '1000054', '1000055') THEN
				SET Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT';
			ELSEIF Environment.Variables.productCode = '102' OR Environment.Variables.productCode = '105' THEN 
				SET Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT';
			ELSEIF Environment.Variables.productCode = '115' AND Environment.Variables.damageCodeId NOT IN ('1000126', '1000127', '1000128', '1000129', '1000130') THEN 
				SET Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT';	
			ELSEIF Environment.Variables.productCode = '103' AND NOT(EXISTS(Environment.Variables.damageCodeId[])) AND EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data.event."primary-cause-of-loss-extcode"[]) THEN 
				SET Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT';	
			ELSEIF Environment.Variables.productCode IN ('103', '107', '108', '109', '110', '114', '115') THEN	
				SET Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'ALIS';
			ELSE
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The product code ' || Environment.Variables.productCode || ' indicated in the payload cannot be processed.');
				RETURN FALSE;	
			END IF;		
		END IF;
		
		
		--MANDATORY FIELDS IN ALL CASES IF THE DESTINATION IS IDIT
		IF Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT' THEN
		   	--event/description
			IF InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.description IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.description) = '' THEN
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter event/description is missing or null.');
				RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--event/primary-cause-of-loss-extcode
			IF InputRoot.JSON.Data.event."primary-cause-of-loss-extcode" IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.JSON.Data.event."primary-cause-of-loss-extcode") = '' THEN	
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter event/primary-cause-of-loss-extcode is missing or null.');
				RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--event/location/country-code
			IF InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.location."country-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.location."country-code") = '' THEN	
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter event/location/country-code is missing or null.');
				RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--event/location/type-desc
			IF InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.location."type-desc" IS NULL OR TRIM(InputRoot.JSON.Data.event.location."type-desc") = '' THEN
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter event/location/type-desc is missing or null.');
				RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--informant/role and informant/personal-id
			DECLARE informant ROW THE(SELECT C FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data."claim-contacts".Item[] AS C WHERE C.role = 'informant');
			IF NOT EXISTS(informant.[]) OR informant."personal-id" IS NULL OR TRIM(informant."personal-id") = '' THEN
			    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter claim-contacts/role or claim-contacts/personal-id of the informant is missing or null.');
			    RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
		END IF;				
		--MANDATORY FIELDS IF THE DESTINATION IS IDIT AND THE PRODUCT CODE = 103 (ETI)
		IF Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT' AND Environment.Variables.productCode = '103' THEN
			FOR claimCoverRef AS InputRoot.JSON.Data."claimed-cover".Item[] DO
			    FOR claimedDamagesRef AS claimCoverRef."claimed-damages".Item[] DO
			    	DECLARE claimedDamagesTravelInfos ROW claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details"."travel-infos";			
			    	DECLARE damageCode CHARACTER claimedDamagesRef."functional-id"; 
			    	IF damageCode IN ('1000005', '1000006', '1000007') THEN
			    		--travel-infos/planned-travel-departure-date
						IF claimedDamagesTravelInfos."planned-travel-departure-date" IS NULL OR TRIM(claimedDamagesTravelInfos."planned-travel-departure-date") = '' THEN
							THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter travel-infos/planned-travel-departure-date is missing or null in one of the claimed damages for the product-code 103
							 						     and damage code ' || damageCode);
							RETURN FALSE;
						END IF;
						--travel-infos/impediment-date	
						IF claimedDamagesTravelInfos."impediment-date" IS NULL OR TRIM(claimedDamagesTravelInfos."impediment-date") = ''  THEN			
							THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter travel-infos/impediment-date is missing or null in one of the claimed damages for the product-code 103
														 and damage code ' || damageCode);
							RETURN FALSE;	
						END IF;
			    	END IF;		    	
					--IF THE DAMAGE CODE ID = 1000039 (Rental Car franchise payment)
					DECLARE damageCodeId ROW claimedDamagesRef."functional-id";
					IF damageCodeId = '1000039' THEN
						DECLARE claimedDamagesCDW ROW claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details".CDW;		
					    --CDW/vehicle-type-one
						IF claimedDamagesCDW."vehicle-type-one" IS NULL THEN
							THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter CDW/vehicle-type-one is missing in the claimed damage with the id 1000039 for the product-code 103.');
							RETURN FALSE;
						END IF;	
					END IF;
			    END FOR;
			END FOR;    					
		END IF;	
		--MANDATORY FIELDS IF THE DESTINATION IS IDIT AND THE PRODUCT CODE = 105 (Bike)
		IF Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT' AND Environment.Variables.productCode = '105' THEN
			FOR claimCoverRef AS InputRoot.JSON.Data."claimed-cover".Item[] DO
			    FOR claimedDamagesRef AS claimCoverRef."claimed-damages".Item[] DO
			    	DECLARE claimedDamagesBike ROW claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details".bike;		
				    --Bike/type
					IF claimedDamagesBike.type IS NULL OR TRIM(claimedDamagesBike.type) = '' THEN	
						THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter Bike/type is missing or null in one of the claimed damages for the product-code 105.');
						RETURN FALSE;
					END IF;
					--Bike/brand
					IF claimedDamagesBike.brand IS NULL OR TRIM(claimedDamagesBike.brand) = '' THEN
						THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter Bike/brand is missing or null in one of the claimed damages for the product-code 105.');
						RETURN FALSE;
					END IF;
					--Bike/25km-more = true, Bike/plate
					IF claimedDamagesBike."25km-more" = 'true' AND COALESCE(TRIM(claimedDamagesBike.plate), '') = '' THEN
                    	THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The input parameter bike/25km-more is true, but the input parameter bike/plate is missing or null in one of the claimed damages for the product-code 105.');
                    	RETURN FALSE;
                    END IF;
			    END FOR;
			END FOR;    	
		END IF;
		--MANDATORY ATTRIBUTE FIELDS AND MANDATORY SUBATTRIBUTE FIELDS FOR THE document-list-guuid ATTRIBUTE IF THE DESTINATION IS IDIT
		IF Environment.Variables.ClaimDestination = 'IDIT' THEN
			--total-estimated-damaged-currency-amount & total-estimated-damaged-currency-code 
			IF (EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data."total-estimated-damaged-currency-amount"[]) OR COALESCE(InputRoot.JSON.Data."total-estimated-damaged-currency-code", '') <> '')
			   AND (NOT(EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data."total-estimated-damaged-currency-amount"[])) OR COALESCE(InputRoot.JSON.Data."total-estimated-damaged-currency-code", '') = '') THEN 
			   		THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('total-estimated-damaged-currency-amount and total-estimated-damaged-currency-code must be present together');
			    	RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--EXTERNAL-CLAIM
			--external-claim-id
			FOR externalClaim AS InputRoot.JSON.Data."external-claim".Item[] DO
				IF COALESCE(externalClaim."external-claim-system", '') <> '' AND COALESCE(externalClaim."external-claim-id", '') = '' THEN 
					THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('external-claim-system is present but external-claim-id is missing');
			    	RETURN FALSE;
				END IF;
			END FOR;
			--ATTRIBUTES
		   	--e-mail	
			DECLARE email ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'e-mail');
			IF NOT EXISTS(email.[]) OR email."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(email."value-code") = '' THEN
			    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The email attribute in the Claim object is missing.');
			    RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--language
			DECLARE language ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'language');
			IF NOT EXISTS(language.[]) OR language."value-code" IS NULL OR NOT language."value-code" IN ('FR', 'DE', 'IT') THEN
			    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The language attribute in the Claim object is missing or is not one of the accepted values (FR, DE, IT).');
			    RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--personal.reference
			DECLARE personalReference ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'personal-reference');	
			IF NOT EXISTS(personalReference.[]) OR personalReference."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(personalReference."value-code") = '' THEN
			    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The personal-reference attribute in the Claim object is missing.');
			    RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--number-of-documents	
			DECLARE numberOfDocuments ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'number-of-documents');
			IF NOT EXISTS(numberOfDocuments.[]) OR numberOfDocuments."value-code" IS NULL THEN
			    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The number-of-documents attribute in the Claim object is missing.');
			    RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--document-list-guuid
			DECLARE documentListGUUID ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'document-list-guuid');
		    IF numberOfDocuments."value-code" > 0 AND (NOT EXISTS(documentListGUUID.[]) OR documentListGUUID."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(documentListGUUID."value-code") = '') THEN    
		        THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The document-list-guuid attribute in the Claim object is missing with the value of the number-of-documents attribute > 0.');
			    RETURN FALSE;
		    END IF;
			--difference between the value of the field number-of-documents and the number of document sub-attributes
			IF numberOfDocuments."value-code" <> CARDINALITY(documentListGUUID."sub-attributes".Item[]) THEN
				THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('There is a difference between the value-code of the number-of-documents attribute and the number of document sub-attributes.');
			    RETURN FALSE;
			END IF;
			--SUBATTRIBUTES
			IF numberOfDocuments."value-code" > 0 THEN   	
				FOR SubAttribute AS documentListGUUID."sub-attributes".Item[] DO
					--document-uuid	
					DECLARE documentUUID ROW THE(SELECT A FROM SubAttribute.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'document-uuid');
					IF NOT EXISTS(documentUUID.[]) OR documentUUID."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(documentUUID."value-code") = '' THEN
					    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The document-uuid sub-attribute is missing for one of the documents in the document-list-guuid attribute.');
					    RETURN FALSE;
					END IF;
					--document-type
					DECLARE documentType ROW THE(SELECT A FROM SubAttribute.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'document-type');
					IF NOT EXISTS(documentType.[]) OR documentType."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(documentType."value-code") = '' THEN
					    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The document-type sub-attribute is missing for one of the documents in the document-list-guuid attribute.');
					    RETURN FALSE;
					END IF;
					--file-name
					DECLARE fileName ROW THE(SELECT A FROM SubAttribute.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'file-name');
					IF NOT EXISTS(fileName.[]) OR fileName."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(fileName."value-code") = '' THEN
					    THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('The file-name sub-attribute is missing for one of the documents in the document-list-guuid attribute.');
					    RETURN FALSE;
					END IF;	
				END FOR;	
			END IF;
		END IF;
		
		SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = UNKNOWN;	
				
		RETURN TRUE;	
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/apps/common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/common_integration_shlib_wrap_message/messageBuild.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_wrap_message


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE messageBuild
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId = InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.Encoding = InputRoot.Properties.Encoding;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.Topic = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.Topic;
		
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN('MQMD') NAME 'MQMD';
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.Format 							= MQFMT_NONE;
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.CodedCharSetId 					= COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.Encoding 						= COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.Encoding, 546);
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.ReplyToQ 						= Environment.Variables.SourceQueue;
		CREATE NEXTSIBLING OF OutputRoot.MQMD DOMAIN ('MQRFH2') NAME 'MQRFH2';	
		
		--set default header with properties values
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Version = MQRFH_VERSION_2;
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Encoding = COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.Encoding, 546);
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)CodedCharSetId = COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Format = MQFMT_STRING; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)NameValueCCSID = COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		--keep message to propagate
		SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])} = InputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])};
		--Set specific header
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.gtid = COALESCE(Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.globalTransactionId,InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.gtid) ; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ptid = COALESCE(Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.parentTransactionId,InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.tid) ; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ltid = COALESCE(Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.localTransactionId,InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.cid) ; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.callerCode = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.originCallerCode = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.sourceSystem = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.targetSystem = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.appName = ApplicationLabel;
		
		--Business identifiers 
		DECLARE businessIdentifiersRef REFERENCE TO InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers".[1];
		WHILE LASTMOVE(businessIdentifiersRef) DO
			SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.{BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR||FIELDNAME(businessIdentifiersRef)} =  FIELDVALUE(businessIdentifiersRef);
			MOVE businessIdentifiersRef NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		
		--User Defined 
		DECLARE userDefinedRef REFERENCE TO InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined.[1];
		WHILE LASTMOVE(userDefinedRef) DO
			SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.{USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR||FIELDNAME(userDefinedRef)} =  FIELDVALUE(userDefinedRef);
			MOVE userDefinedRef NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		
		-- filters
		DECLARE filtersRef REFERENCE TO InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.filters.[1] ;
		WHILE LASTMOVE(filtersRef) DO
			SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.{FILTERSEPARATOR||FIELDNAME(filtersRef)} =  FIELDVALUE(filtersRef);
			MOVE filtersRef NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/apps/common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/common_integration_shlib_wrap_message/messageExtract.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_wrap_message
DECLARE BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR CHARACTER 'BI';
DECLARE USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR CHARACTER 'UD';
DECLARE FILTERSEPARATOR CHARACTER 'FT';

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE messageExtract
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		--get default header with properties values
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.Encoding = InputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Encoding ;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)CodedCharSetId ;
		--keep message to propagate
		SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])} = InputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])};
		
		
		--Set specific header
		--Set monitoring ID only for Global transaction ID (=gtid) and Correlation ID (=ptid)
		SET Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.globalTransactionId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.gtid ;
		SET Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.parentTransactionId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ptid ;
		--SET Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.localTransactionId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ltid ;
		
		--save all message for record
		SET Environment.Variables.MessageIn = InputRoot ;
				
		--Get specific MQ field
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.Topic = FIELDVALUE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.mqps.Top);
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.SourceQueue = InputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue;
		SET Environment.Variables.SourceQueue = InputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue;
		SET Environment.Variables.ReplyToQ = InputRoot.MQMD.ReplyToQ ;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.callerCode ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.originCallerCode ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.sourceSystem ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.targetSystem ;
				
		--Set in LocalEnvironment Business identifiers and Userdefined saved in headers
		DECLARE mqrfh2Headers REFERENCE TO InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.[1];
		WHILE LASTMOVE(mqrfh2Headers) DO
			IF STARTSWITH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers),BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR) THEN
				DECLARE bitmp CHARACTER SUBSTRING(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers) FROM LENGTH(BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR)+1 FOR LENGTH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers))-LENGTH(BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR));
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers".{bitmp} = mqrfh2Headers ;
			ELSEIF STARTSWITH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers),USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR) THEN
				DECLARE udtmp CHARACTER SUBSTRING(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers) FROM LENGTH(USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR)+1 FOR LENGTH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers))-LENGTH(USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR));
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined.{udtmp} = mqrfh2Headers ;
			ELSEIF STARTSWITH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers),FILTERSEPARATOR) THEN
				DECLARE fttmp CHARACTER SUBSTRING(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers) FROM LENGTH(FILTERSEPARATOR)+1 FOR LENGTH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers))-LENGTH(FILTERSEPARATOR));
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.filters.{fttmp} = mqrfh2Headers ;
			END IF;
			MOVE mqrfh2Headers NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/apps/common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/common_integration_shlib_wrap_message/messageRecover.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_wrap_message
DECLARE runtimeEnvironment EXTERNAL CHARACTER 'DEV';


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE messageRecover
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		DELETE FIELD OutputRoot.Properties;
		FOR Field AS Environment.Variables.MessageIn.*[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN(FIELDNAME(Field)) NAME FIELDNAME(Field);
			SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(Field)} = Field;
		END FOR;
		--define reply queue + app name
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.SourceQueue = OutputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue ;
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.appReplayName = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.runtimeEnvironment = runtimeEnvironment;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-policies-reader/apps/common-integration-shlib-policies-reader/Shlib_PoliciesReader/GetUDPEsqlFunction.esql

BROKER SCHEMA Shlib_PoliciesReader

CREATE FUNCTION gestionPolicy( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER ) RETURNS CHARACTER
BEGIN
	DECLARE prop CHAR getPolicyPropertyWithOutError(policyName, propertyName);
	if prop = 'POLICY_NOT_FOUND' OR prop = 'PROPERTY_NOT_FOUND' THEN 
		THROW USER EXCEPTION CATALOG 'MyCatalog' MESSAGE 4004 VALUES(policyName || '/' || propertyName || ' not found');
	end if;
	return prop;

END;
CREATE PROCEDURE getAllPolicyProperties (IN PolicyProjectName CHAR,IN PolicyName CHAR, IN rowRef REFERENCE )  
BEGIN
	/* 
		exemple d'appel  
		call getAllPolicyProperties('DigitalIntakePolicies','BALOISEDIG',OutputRoot.JSON.Data.test);
	*/
	DECLARE properties CHAR getAllPolicyPropertiesJava('{' || PolicyProjectName || '}:' ||  PolicyName);
	DECLARE RowTempo ROW;
	DECLARE dataAsBit BIT  CAST(properties AS BIT CCSID 1208);
	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RowTempo DOMAIN 'JSON' PARSE(dataAsBit CCSID 1208);
	set rowRef = RowTempo.JSON.Data;
	
END;
CREATE FUNCTION getPolicyProperty( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getPolicyProperty";

CREATE FUNCTION getPolicyPropertyWithOutError( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getPolicyPropertyWithOutError";

CREATE FUNCTION getAllPolicyPropertiesJava( IN policyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getAllPolicyProperties";

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/buildError_getClaims.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE buildError_getClaims	
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN		
		-- parse the exception list tree (if there is one) in order to fill the http response returned afterwards
		IF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
			CALL parseExceptionList(InputExceptionList, Environment.Variables.ExceptionNumber, Environment.Variables.ExceptionMessage, Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace);
		END IF;
		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code"[]) THEN
			DECLARE jsonAsBlob BLOB;
			IF EXISTS (InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB[]) THEN
				SET jsonAsBlob  = InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB;
			ELSEIF EXISTS (InputRoot.JSON.Data[]) THEN
				SET jsonAsBlob  = ASBITSTREAM(InputRoot.JSON.Data CCSID COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId,1208));
			END IF;
			DECLARE jsonAsChar CHAR CAST(jsonAsBlob AS CHAR CCSID 1208);
			SET Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace = jsonAsChar;
		END IF;
		-- check provider, if empty this means that it's necessarily ACE that is causing the error 
		SET Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName, 'ACE');
		
		-- check if the environment contains an http status error code to return (if not by default we return 500) 
		IF Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode IS NULL OR CARDINALITY(Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode[]) = 0 THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code",500);
			IF Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName = 'APIC' AND Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode = 401 THEN
				SET Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp = 'ACETECH0011';
			END IF;
		END IF;
		-- create the http response (http status & body)
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHARACTER FORMAT 'I');
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName || ' : ' || CAST(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderHttpReturnCode, Environment.Variables.HttpReturnCode) AS CHARACTER);
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName || ' : ' || COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionMessage, COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Line",'No exception message !'));
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.refAppError = Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.gtid = Environment.Variables.logging.envelope.GTID;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.ExceptionStackTrace, '');

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/buildErrorGetClaimByClaimId.esql


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE buildErrorGetClaimByClaimId
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode[]) THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName = 'APIC';
		ELSE
			SET Environment.Variables.ErrorProviderName = 'IDIT';
		END IF;
		SET Environment.Variables.ErrorRefApp = 'ACEFONC0020';
		
		RETURN TRUE;		
	END;	
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/BuildMessageForArchive.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE BuildMessageForArchive
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN		
		IF Environment.Variables.numberOfDocuments > 0 THEN			
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.documentListGUUID = Environment.Variables.documentListGUUID;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.numberOfDocuments = Environment.Variables.numberOfDocuments;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.personalReference = Environment.Variables.policyHolderPersonalReference;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.policyNumber = Environment.Variables.policyNumber;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.claimNumber = Environment.Variables."claim-number";	
			
			--Logs
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05625';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = UNKNOWN;
		    
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction.CLAIM_NUMBER = Environment.Variables."claim-number";	
		    
		    SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		    SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;	      
			
			RETURN TRUE;
		ELSE
			RETURN FALSE;	
		END IF;			
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/BuildMessageForEmail.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE BuildMessageForEmail
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN				
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.email = Environment.Variables.email;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.claimNumber = Environment.Variables."claim-number";
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.language = Environment.Variables.language;
		IF Environment.Variables.IDITerror = TRUE THEN
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.error = TRUE;
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.errorHttpCode = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode, '500');
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.errorHttpMessage = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage, 'Internal Server Error');
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.numberOfDocuments = Environment.Variables.numberOfDocuments;
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.documentListGUUID = Environment.Variables.documentListGUUID;
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.documentList[] = Environment.Variables.documentList[];
		    SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.keyValuesOfOnlineForm = Environment.Variables.keyValuesOfOnlineForm;
		ELSE
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.error = FALSE;    	
		END IF;		
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.policyNumber = Environment.Variables.policyNumber;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.personalReference = Environment.Variables.informantPersonalReference;			
		
		--Logs
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05625';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = UNKNOWN;
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = UNKNOWN;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = UNKNOWN;                    
	    
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction.CLAIM_NUMBER = Environment.Variables."claim-number";	 
	    
	    SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;	     
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/CheckIBANInIDITOrUpdate.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CheckIBANInIDITOrUpdate
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE bankAccountInIDIT ROW THE(SELECT B FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.bankAccount.Item[] AS B WHERE B.ibanReference = Environment.Variables.IBANClient);
		IF EXISTS(bankAccountInIDIT.[]) THEN  --IBAN of contact exists in IDIT
			SET Environment.Variables.bankAccountID = CAST(bankAccountInIDIT.id AS CHARACTER);
			
			--logs
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables.In.XCallerCode;
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = UNKNOWN;
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05625';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1';
			RETURN FALSE;
		ELSE                                  --IBAN of contact doesn't exist in IDIT 
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;	
			
			DECLARE bankAccountList REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data.bankAccount;
            SET bankAccountList.Item[] = SELECT * FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.bankAccount.Item[] AS B WHERE B.type.id = 1;
			
			DECLARE bankAccountRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot.JSON.Data.(JSON.Array)bankAccount AS bankAccountRef NAME 'Item';
			
			DECLARE typeRef REFERENCE TO bankAccountRef;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF bankAccountRef AS typeRef NAME 'type';
			SET typeRef.id = 1;
			
			DECLARE bankCountryRef REFERENCE TO bankAccountRef;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF bankAccountRef AS bankCountryRef TYPE Name NAME 'country';
			SET bankCountryRef.extCode = SUBSTRING(Environment.Variables.IBANClient FROM 1 FOR 2);
			SET bankAccountRef.ibanReference = Environment.Variables.IBANClient;
			
			--logs
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables.In.XCallerCode;
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = UNKNOWN;
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05625';
		    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		    
			RETURN TRUE;
		END IF;	
		
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/CheckIBANInPayload.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CheckIBANInPayload
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		
		DECLARE IBAN ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'iban');	
		IF NOT EXISTS(IBAN.[]) OR IBAN."value-code" IS NULL OR TRIM(IBAN."value-code") = '' THEN			
		    PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1';
			RETURN FALSE;
		ELSE
			SET Environment.Variables.IBANClient = IBAN."value-code";			
			RETURN TRUE;    
		END IF;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/CreateClaim_BuildFailureMessage.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CreateClaim_BuildFailureMessage
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE exceptionList REFERENCE TO InputExceptionList.RecoverableException;
		DECLARE refRecoverableException REFERENCE TO exceptionList;
		CALL getUserExceptionCatalog(exceptionList, refRecoverableException);		
		IF EXISTS(refRecoverableException.SocketTimeoutException.*[]) THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode = '504';
		    SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage = 'ACE : Gateway Timeout';
		    SET Environment.Variables.Error.moreInformation = 'The maximum response time has been exceeded.';
		ELSE			
		    SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode = '500';
		    SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage = 'ACE : ' || refRecoverableException.Catalog || ': ' || CAST(refRecoverableException.Number AS CHARACTER);
		    DECLARE errorMessage CHARACTER refRecoverableException."Text"; 
		    DECLARE errorElement INTEGER 0;
		    FOR data AS refRecoverableException.Insert[] DO
			    SET errorElement = errorElement + 1;
			    SET errorMessage = errorMessage || '; &' || CAST(errorElement AS CHARACTER) || '=' || data."Text";
		    END FOR;	
		    SET Environment.Variables.Error.moreInformation = errorMessage;	
		END IF;			
		
		THROW USER EXCEPTION; 		
		RETURN FALSE;	
	END;
	
	CREATE PROCEDURE getUserExceptionCatalog(IN root REFERENCE, INOUT outRoot REFERENCE) BEGIN
		DECLARE element REFERENCE TO root;
		MOVE outRoot TO element;
		MOVE element TO element.RecoverableException;
		IF LASTMOVE(element) IS TRUE THEN
			CALL getUserExceptionCatalog(element, outRoot);
		END IF;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/CreateClaim_ErrorResponseIdit.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CreateClaim_ErrorResponseIditAsync
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN		
		IF InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB IS NOT NULL THEN
			DECLARE errorMessage ROW;
		    CREATE LASTCHILD OF errorMessage DOMAIN('JSON') PARSE(CAST(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS BLOB));
		    DECLARE errorMessageString CHARACTER CAST(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS CHARACTER CCSID 1208);
		
		    IF errorMessage.JSON.Data.httpCode IS NOT NULL THEN
		        SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode = errorMessage.JSON.Data.httpCode;
		        SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage = 'APIC : ' || errorMessage.JSON.Data.httpMessage;
	            SET Environment.Variables.Error.moreInformation = errorMessage.JSON.Data.moreInformation;
		    ELSE
		        SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode = errorMessage.JSON.Data.status;
		        SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage = 'IDIT : ' || errorMessage.JSON.Data.title;
		        DECLARE httpMessageRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage;
		        IF CARDINALITY(errorMessage.JSON.Data."invalid-params".Item[]) > 0 THEN
		        	SET httpMessageRef = httpMessageRef || ', ' || 'invalid-parameter-name: ';
		        	FOR invalidParamRef as errorMessage.JSON.Data."invalid-params".Item[] DO
		        		SET httpMessageRef = httpMessageRef || invalidParamRef.name || ', ';
		        	END FOR;
		        	SET httpMessageRef = LEFT(httpMessageRef,LENGTH(httpMessageRef)-2);
		        END IF;
	            SET Environment.Variables.Error.moreInformation = errorMessageString;
		    END IF;
		ELSE	
			SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode = '500';
			SET Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage = 'ACE : Internal Server Error';
	        SET Environment.Variables.Error.moreInformation = 'Error Calling IDIT';
		END IF;	
		
		THROW USER EXCEPTION; 		
		RETURN FALSE;	
	END;
	
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/CreateClaim_mappingClaimToIdit.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CreateClaim_mappingClaimToIdit
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE inRef 	REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE outRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE pattern CHARACTER '##0.00';
		
		SET outRef.externalPolicyNumber					= inRef."policy-number";
		SET outRef.claimEventDate 						= inRef.event.date; -- Le format to check
		SET outRef.claimEventDescription				= inRef.event.description;
		
		--claimCauseOfLossVO
		DECLARE claimCauseOfLossVORef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef AS claimCauseOfLossVORef TYPE Name NAME 'claimCauseOfLossVO'; 
		SET claimCauseOfLossVORef.extCode				= inRef.event."primary-cause-of-loss-extcode";
		--claimEventLocationIBO
		DECLARE claimEventLocationIBORef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef AS claimEventLocationIBORef TYPE Name NAME 'claimEventLocationIBO';
		DECLARE countryVORef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimEventLocationIBORef AS countryVORef TYPE Name NAME 'countryVO';
		SET countryVORef.extCode						= inRef.event.location."country-code";
		DECLARE locationTypeRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimEventLocationIBORef AS locationTypeRef TYPE Name NAME 'locationType';
		SET locationTypeRef.extCode						= inRef.event.location."type-desc";
		
		--claimInformantIBO
		DECLARE claimInformantIBORef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef AS claimInformantIBORef TYPE Name NAME 'claimInformantIBO';
		DECLARE informantTypeRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimInformantIBORef AS informantTypeRef TYPE Name NAME 'informantType';
		DECLARE isPolicyHolderPayment BOOLEAN FALSE;
		IF Environment.Variables.policyHolderPersonalReference <> Environment.Variables.informantPersonalReference THEN
			SET informantTypeRef.extCode = 'family_member';
		ELSE
			SET informantTypeRef.extCode = 'policyholder';
		END IF;
		SET claimInformantIBORef.contactExternalNumber = Environment.Variables.informantPersonalReference;
		--relatedExternalClaims
		DECLARE relatedExternalClaimsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		FOR externalClaimsRef AS inRef."external-claim".Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)relatedExternalClaims AS relatedExternalClaimsRef NAME 'Item';
			SET relatedExternalClaimsRef.externalSystem	= externalClaimsRef."external-claim-system";
			SET relatedExternalClaimsRef.claimNumber	= externalClaimsRef."external-claim-id";
			IF COALESCE(externalClaimsRef."external-claim-status-extCode", '') <> '' THEN		
				SET relatedExternalClaimsRef.claimStatus.extCode = externalClaimsRef."external-claim-status-extCode";
			END IF;	
		END FOR;	
		--totalEstimatedClaim
		SET outRef.totalEstimatedDamage = CAST(inRef."total-estimated-damaged-currency-amount" AS DECIMAL);
		--totalEstimatedDamageCurrency
		IF COALESCE(inRef."total-estimated-damaged-currency-code", '') <> '' THEN
			SET outRef.totalEstimatedDamageCurrency.extCode = inRef."total-estimated-damaged-currency-code";
		END IF;
		--TCSclaimChannel
		IF COALESCE(inRef."channel-extCode", '') <> '' THEN
			SET outRef.TCSclaimChannel.extCode = inRef."channel-extCode";
		END IF;
		--claimantIBOs
		DECLARE functionalId CHARACTER '';
		DECLARE frame CHARACTER '';
		DECLARE claimantIBOsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;		
		FOR claimedCoverRef AS inRef."claimed-cover".Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)claimantIBOs AS claimantIBOsRef NAME 'Item';		
			-- Add policy holder "policy-Holder"
			SET claimantIBOsRef.contactExternalNumber	= Environment.Variables.policyHolderPersonalReference;
			DECLARE claimantLiabilityRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimantIBOsRef AS claimantLiabilityRef TYPE Name NAME 'claimantLiability';	
			SET claimantLiabilityRef.extCode			= 'unknown'; -- value default in Idit
			DECLARE claimantSideRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimantIBOsRef AS claimantSideRef TYPE Name NAME 'claimantSide';
			SET claimantSideRef.extCode					= 'policyholder';
			--NIPO-6593
			DECLARE damageCodeRef,damagesRef,damagedObjectsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;	
			--claimantIBOs/damagedObjects/damageCode
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimantIBOsRef.(JSON.Array)damagedObjects AS damagedObjectsRef NAME 'Item';		
			FOR claimedDamagesRef AS claimedCoverRef."claimed-damages".Item[] DO											  
                -- Vérifier si l'élément existe déjà dans la liste unique
                DECLARE isDuplicate BOOLEAN FALSE;
                check:BEGIN	
                		FOR claimantIBOsItem AS outRef.claimantIBOs.Item[] DO
                			FOR damagedObjectsItem AS claimantIBOsItem.damagedObjects.Item[] DO 				    	   			
							    FOR damagesItem AS damagedObjectsItem.damages.Item[] DO
							        IF damagesItem.damageCode.id = claimedDamagesRef."functional-id" THEN
							            SET isDuplicate = TRUE;
							            LEAVE check;
							        END IF;
							    END FOR;
                			END FOR;
                		END FOR;	    
			    END;		    
			    -- Si the damageCode existe, does not add it.
			    IF isDuplicate = FALSE THEN
			    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsRef.(JSON.Array)damages AS damagesRef NAME 'Item';
			    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagesRef AS damageCodeRef TYPE Name NAME 'damageCode'; 
				    SET damageCodeRef.id 			= claimedDamagesRef."functional-id";
				    --No use for the mapping, just for the next step "accidentCircumstancesQuestionnaire"
				    SET functionalId 				= claimedDamagesRef."functional-id";
				     --damages/estimatedDamageAmount et damages/estimatedDamageCurrency				    
					FOR reimbursementRef AS claimedDamagesRef.reimbursement.Item[] DO					
						SET damagesRef.estimatedDamageAmount	=  CAST(CAST(reimbursementRef."estimated-amount" AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
						DECLARE estimatedDamageCurrencyRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagesRef AS estimatedDamageCurrencyRef TYPE Name NAME 'estimatedDamageCurrency';
						SET estimatedDamageCurrencyRef.extCode	= reimbursementRef.currency;
						SET damagesRef.preferredContactBankAccountID = Environment.Variables.bankAccountID;
					END FOR;
					IF Environment.Variables.policyHolderPersonalReference <> Environment.Variables.ibanHolderPersonalReference THEN
						SET damagesRef.automaticPaymentTo.extCode = 'beneficiary';
					ELSE
						SET damagesRef.automaticPaymentTo.extCode = 'policyholder';
					END IF;
				END IF;
				--claimantIBOs/damagedObjects/accidentCircumstancesQuestionnaire
			    IF functionalId = '1000039' THEN
			    	DECLARE questionnaireRef,questionnaireVORef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
				    CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsRef.(JSON.Array)accidentCircumstancesQuestionnaire AS questionnaireRef NAME 'Item';
				    CREATE LASTCHILD OF questionnaireRef AS questionnaireVORef TYPE Name NAME 'questionnaireVO';
				    SET questionnaireVORef.id 				= 1000001;
				    DECLARE questionnaireLineIBOsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;			  
				    CREATE LASTCHILD OF questionnaireRef.(JSON.Array)questionnaireLineIBOs AS questionnaireLineIBOsRef NAME 'Item';
				    DECLARE questionnaireLineVORef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
				    CREATE LASTCHILD OF questionnaireLineIBOsRef AS questionnaireLineVORef TYPE Name NAME 'questionnaireLineVO';
				    SET questionnaireLineVORef.id				= 1000005;
				    IF claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details".CDW."vehicle-type-one" IS NOT NULL THEN 
				    	SET questionnaireLineIBOsRef.answerId		= claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details".CDW."vehicle-type-one";
				    END IF;
				    CREATE LASTCHILD OF questionnaireRef.(JSON.Array)questionnaireLineIBOs AS questionnaireLineIBOsRef NAME 'Item';
				    --DECLARE questionnaireLineIBOsItem2Ref REFERENCE TO questionnaireLineIBOsRef.Item[2];
			    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF questionnaireLineIBOsRef AS questionnaireLineVORef TYPE Name NAME 'questionnaireLineVO';
				    SET questionnaireLineVORef.id				= 1000006;
				    IF claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details".CDW."damage-type-one" IS NOT NULL THEN 
				    	SET questionnaireLineIBOsRef.answerId		= claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details".CDW."damage-type-one";
				    END IF;
			    END IF;
				--claimantIBOs/damagedObjects/damagedItems
				DECLARE damagedItemsRef,itemTypeRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
				IF EXISTS(claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details"."damaged-items"[]) THEN
					FOR damagedItemsTCSRef AS claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details"."damaged-items".Item[] DO
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsRef.(JSON.Array)damagedItems AS damagedItemsRef NAME 'Item'; 
				        CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedItemsRef AS itemTypeRef TYPE Name NAME 'itemType';
						SET itemTypeRef.extCode							= damagedItemsTCSRef.extCode;					
						SET damagedItemsRef.isActive					= damagedItemsTCSRef.isActive;
						SET damagedItemsRef.desc						= damagedItemsTCSRef.description;
						SET damagedItemsRef.purchaseValue				= damagedItemsTCSRef."purchase-value";
					END FOR;
				END IF;
				DECLARE damagedObjectDetailsTCSRef REFERENCE TO claimedDamagesRef."damaged-objects-details";
				DECLARE travelInfosRef ROW damagedObjectDetailsTCSRef."travel-infos";
				IF EXISTS(travelInfosRef.*[]) THEN					
			    	--travelDates
			    	DECLARE travelDatesRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			    	IF NOT EXISTS(outRef.travelDates.*[]) THEN
				    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef AS travelDatesRef TYPE Name NAME 'travelDates';
				    	SET travelDatesRef.travelBookingDate	= damagedObjectDetailsTCSRef."travel-infos"."travel-booking-date";
				    	SET travelDatesRef.travelDepartureDate	= damagedObjectDetailsTCSRef."travel-infos"."planned-travel-departure-date";
				    	SET travelDatesRef.travelReturnDate		= damagedObjectDetailsTCSRef."travel-infos"."planned-travel-return-date";	
					   	SET travelDatesRef.dateOfImpediment		= damagedObjectDetailsTCSRef."travel-infos"."impediment-date";
			    	END IF;			    				    	
				END IF;
			END FOR;
		END FOR;
		--insuredBikeDetails						
		FOR claimedCoverInRef AS inRef."claimed-cover".Item[] DO
			FOR claimedDamagesInRef AS claimedCoverInRef."claimed-damages".Item[] DO
				DECLARE damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef REFERENCE TO claimedDamagesInRef."damaged-objects-details";
				DECLARE bikeRef ROW damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike;
				DECLARE isSameBike BOOLEAN FALSE;
				-- Check the bike object exists
				IF EXISTS(bikeRef.*[]) THEN
						--Avoid duplicate bikes						 
						checkIfSameBike: BEGIN
							FOR existeBike AS outRef.insuredBikeDetails.Item[] DO						
				      			IF damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike.frame = existeBike.frame THEN
				      				SET isSameBike = TRUE;
				      				LEAVE checkIfSameBike; 
				      			END IF;
							END FOR;
						END;
													
		      		IF  isSameBike = FALSE THEN -- Add bike detail.		
						--insuredBikeDetails
						DECLARE insuredBikeDetailsRef,bikeTypeRef,bikeBrandRef,bikeColorRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)insuredBikeDetails AS insuredBikeDetailsRef NAME 'Item';
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF insuredBikeDetailsRef AS bikeTypeRef TYPE Name NAME 'bikeType';					
						SET bikeTypeRef.extCode						= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike.type;
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF insuredBikeDetailsRef AS bikeBrandRef TYPE Name NAME 'bikeBrand';
						SET bikeBrandRef.extCode					= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike.brand;
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF insuredBikeDetailsRef AS bikeColorRef TYPE Name NAME 'bikeColor';
						SET bikeColorRef.extCode					= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike.color;
						SET insuredBikeDetailsRef.frame				= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike.frame;
						SET insuredBikeDetailsRef.reachesOver25KMperHour				= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike."25km-more";
						SET insuredBikeDetailsRef.plate				= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike.plate;
						SET insuredBikeDetailsRef.purchaseDate				= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike."purchase-date";
						SET insuredBikeDetailsRef.firstRegistrationDate	= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike."first-reg-date";
						SET insuredBikeDetailsRef.bikeValue			= damagedObjectDetailsTCSInRef.bike."purchase-value";										
					END IF;
				END IF;	
			END FOR;	
		END FOR;	
		--listOfTravelers
		FOR travelersTCSRef AS inRef.travelers.Item[] DO
			DECLARE travelersRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)listOfTravelers As travelersRef NAME 'Item'; 
			SET travelersRef.firstName						= travelersTCSRef."first-name";
			SET	travelersRef.lastName						= travelersTCSRef."last-name";
			IF travelersTCSRef."category-description" IS NOT NULL AND travelersTCSRef."category-description" <> '' THEN
				DECLARE travelerCategoryRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
				CREATE LASTCHILD OF travelersRef AS travelerCategoryRef TYPE Name NAME 'travelerCategory';
				SET travelerCategoryRef.extCode				= travelersTCSRef."category-description";
			END IF;
		END FOR;
		IF Environment.Variables.policyHolderPersonalReference <> Environment.Variables.ibanHolderPersonalReference THEN
			DECLARE claimContactsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)claimContacts AS claimContactsRef NAME 'Item';
			SET claimContactsRef.contactExternalNumber = Environment.Variables.ibanHolderPersonalReference;
			SET claimContactsRef.claimContactRole.extCode = 'beneficiary';
		END IF;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE CreateClaim_PostClaim_PreparerCallIdit
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
		BEGIN

			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader.Accept = 'application/json, application/xml, text/html';
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
			
			DECLARE URLAPI CHARACTER '';
			DECLARE PATHAPI CHARACTER '';
			
			SET URLAPI = Environment.Variables.policy.apicHost;
			SET PATHAPI = Environment.Variables.policy.claimPost;
			
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables.logging.envelope.GTID;
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Request-ID" = UUIDASCHAR;
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Correlation-ID" =  Environment.Variables.logging.envelope.TID;
			
       
	        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URLAPI || PATHAPI;
	       	SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'POST';
	       	
	       	--Logs
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05614';
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit';
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = URLAPI || PATHAPI;
		
			RETURN TRUE;
		END;

END MODULE;

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE GetClaimByPersonalId_prepareCallForIDIT
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
		BEGIN
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader.Accept = 'application/json';

			DECLARE URLAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
			DECLARE PATHAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{insurance-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndPointsPolicy','path-insurance-policies-integration-technical-idit-search-search-claims'), '');
			DECLARE PERSONALID CHARACTER Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts"."personal-id";
			DECLARE NUMBEROFYEARS CHARACTER Environment.Variables.claim."number-of-years";

			-- Set personal-id
			SET PATHAPI = REPLACE(PATHAPI,'{externalContactNumber}',PERSONALID);
			-- Set number-of-years
			IF NUMBEROFYEARS IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(NUMBEROFYEARS) <> '' THEN
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.QueryString.years = NUMBEROFYEARS;
			END IF;

	        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URLAPI || PATHAPI;
	       	SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'GET';

			RETURN TRUE;
		END;
END MODULE;
