File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/GetInputData.esql


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE GetInputData
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		--Get the input parameter personal-id/policyHolder 
		FOR claimContactsRef as InputRoot.JSON.Data."claim-contacts".Item[] DO
			SET Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts"."personal-id" = claimContactsRef."personal-id";
		END FOR;
	
        --Get the number of years present, if it is present, it is just a number
		FOR operationalStatusesRef as InputRoot.JSON.Data."operational-statuses".Item[] DO
			FOR statusRef as operationalStatusesRef.status.Item[] DO
				FOR complementariesRef AS statusRef.complementaries.Item[] DO 
					IF complementariesRef."operational-status-compl-code" = 'number-of-years' THEN					
						SET Environment.Variables.claim."number-of-years" = complementariesRef."operational-status-compl-info";
					END IF;
				END FOR;	
			END FOR;		
		END FOR;
		
		RETURN TRUE;	
	END;
END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/globalFunctions.esql

/* Procedure to get last exepstion list field
	IN REFERENCE exlist_in 'The exeption list reference'
	OUT INTEGER number_out 'Last bips exeption number'
	OUT REFERENCE text_out 'Last bips exeption text'
	OUT REFERENCE stacktrace_out 'Complete stacktrace'
	Return CHARACTER*/
CREATE FUNCTION parseExceptionList(IN exlist_in REFERENCE, OUT number_out INTEGER, OUT text_out CHARACTER, OUT stacktrace_out CHARACTER)
BEGIN
	-- The complete statck trace of the exception (result of the loop into the tree) 
	DECLARE completeStackTrace CHARACTER '';
	-- Reference on the ExceptionList tree
	DECLARE refExceptionList REFERENCE TO exlist_in.*[1];
	-- Loop into the tree through the ExceptionList children
	WHILE refExceptionList.Number IS NOT NULL DO
		SET completeStackTrace = CAST(refExceptionList.Number AS CHARACTER) || ':' || refExceptionList.Text || ':' || COALESCE(refExceptionList.Label, '') || '
		'|| completeStackTrace ;
		MOVE refExceptionList LASTCHILD;
	END WHILE;
	-- Return the last exepction number and text and the complete stack trace
	SET number_out = refExceptionList.Number;
	SET text_out = refExceptionList.Text;
	SET stacktrace_out = completeStackTrace;
END;

/* Function to transform sfsc status code to bom status
	IN CHARACTER statusCode 'Status code from sfsc'
	Return CHARACTER*/
CREATE FUNCTION StatusCodeToStatus(IN statusCode CHARACTER) RETURNS CHARACTER
BEGIN
	CASE statusCode
		WHEN '01' THEN
			RETURN 'Active';
		WHEN '02' THEN
			RETURN 'Cancelled';
		WHEN '03' THEN
			RETURN 'Expired';
		WHEN '04' THEN
			RETURN 'Expired';
		WHEN '05' THEN
			RETURN 'Expired';
		WHEN '06' THEN
			RETURN 'Expired';
	ELSE 
		RETURN '';
	END CASE;
END;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/health_Compute.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE health_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CALL CopyMessageHeaders();
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/health+json';
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.status = 'pass';
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.api = 'insurance-claims-api-claims';
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as CHAR FORMAT 'I');
		DECLARE "X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code" INT COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code", 500);
		
		CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data.(JSON.Array)checks.Item;
		DECLARE RefOut REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data.checks.Item;
		SET RefOut.component = 'IDIT HealthCheck';
		
		IF NOT("X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code" = 200) THEN
			SET RefOut.status = 'error';
			IF EXISTS(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB[]) THEN
				SET RefOut.details = cast(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS CHARACTER CCSID InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId ENCODING InputRoot.Properties.Encoding);
			ELSEIF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
				DECLARE tmp ROW;
				CREATE LASTCHILD OF tmp DOMAIN('JSON') NAME 'JSON';
				SET tmp.JSON.Data.ExceptionList = InputExceptionList;
				SET tmp.BLOB = ASBITSTREAM(tmp.JSON.Data.ExceptionList OPTIONS FolderBitStream CCSID 1208);
				SET RefOut.details = CAST(tmp.BLOB AS CHAR CCSID 1208);
			END IF;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 500;
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.status = 'warn';
		ELSE
			SET RefOut.status = 'pass';
			SET RefOut.details = cast(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB as CHARACTER CCSID InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId ENCODING InputRoot.Properties.Encoding);
			
		END IF;		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
	
	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/health_PrepareCallForIDIT.esql

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE health_PrepareCallForIDIT
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET Environment.variables."X-Caller-Code" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code";
		DECLARE URLAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(getPolicyProperty('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
		DECLARE PATHAPI CHARACTER COALESCE(getPolicyProperty('{insurance-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndPointsPolicy','path-insurance-policies-integration-idit-system-get-app-details'), '');
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URLAPI || PATHAPI;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.Timeout  = 15;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

      <connections xmi:type="eflow:FCMConnection" xmi:id="FCMConnection_2" targetNode="OutTerminal.Output_1" sourceNode="FCMComposite_1_1" sourceTerminalName="OutTerminal.Output" targetTerminalName="InTerminal.in"/>
    </composition>
    <propertyOrganizer/>
    <stickyBoard/>
  </eClassifiers>
</ecore:EPackage>

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/mappingResponseIditGetClaimByClaimId.esql

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE mappingResponseIditGetClaimByClaimId
	
CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE inRef 	REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE outRef,externalClaimRef,travelersRef,claimContactsRef,damagedItemsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE pattern CHARACTER '##0.00';
		
		DECLARE productCode CHAR  
		CASE
			WHEN STARTSWITH(UPPER(inRef.targetClaimNumber),'SI-') THEN SUBSTRING(UPPER(inRef.targetClaimNumber) FROM 6 FOR 3)
			ELSE SUBSTRING(UPPER(inRef.targetClaimNumber) FROM 3 FOR 3)
		END;	
			
		--This block are the base informations for a claim
		SET outRef."technical-id" 		= inRef.id;
		SET outRef."claim-number" 		= inRef.targetClaimNumber;
		SET outRef."claim-number" 		= inRef.targetClaimNumber;
		SET outRef.status		 		= inRef.claimStatus.id;
		SET outRef."status-desc" 		= inRef.claimStatus.desc;
		SET outRef."update-version" 	= inRef.updateVersion;
		SET outRef."notification-date" 	= SUBSTRING(inRef.signedNotificationDate BEFORE 'T');
		SET outRef."policy-number" 		= inRef.externalPolicyNumber;
		SET outRef.type					= inRef.claimFNOLind.desc;
		SET outRef."policy-number"		= inRef.externalPolicyNumber;
		SET outRef."closure-date"		= SUBSTRING(inRef.claimCloseDate BEFORE 'T');
		SET outRef."status-reason-code"	= inRef.claimStatusReasonTypeVO.id;
		SET outRef."status-reason-desc"	= inRef.claimStatusReasonTypeVO.desc;
		
		SET outRef."state-reason-code"	= inRef.claimState.id;
		SET outRef."state-reason-desc"	= inRef.claimState.desc;
		DECLARE claimantIBOsItem1Ref REFERENCE TO inRef."claimantIBOs".Item[1];
		DECLARE claimantStateRef ROW claimantIBOsItem1Ref.claimantState;
        IF EXISTS(claimantStateRef.*[]) THEN -- Check l'object claimantState existe 
			SET outRef."claimant-state"	= claimantStateRef.desc; 
        END IF;
        DECLARE claimantStatusReasonRef ROW claimantIBOsItem1Ref.claimantStatusReason;
        IF EXISTS(claimantStatusReasonRef.*[]) THEN -- Check l'object claimantStatusReason existe 
			SET outRef."claimant-status-reason"	= claimantStatusReasonRef.desc; 
        END IF;		
		SET outRef."no-policy" = inRef.claimWithoutPolicy;	
		SET outRef."channel"   = inRef.claimChannel.extCode;
		
		--totals amounts
		DECLARE damagedObjectsItem1Ref REFERENCE TO claimantIBOsItem1Ref.damagedObjects.Item[1];	    
	    SET outRef."total-approved-claim-payment-amount"      = CAST(CAST(damagedObjectsItem1Ref.totalApprovedClaimPaymentsAmountPerDamagedObject AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
	    SET outRef."total-unapproved-claim-payment-amount"    = CAST(CAST(damagedObjectsItem1Ref.totalUnapprovedClaimPaymentsAmountPerDamagedObject AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
	    SET outRef."total-approved-recovery-request-amount"   = CAST(CAST(damagedObjectsItem1Ref.totalApprovedRecoveryRequestsAmountPerDamagedObject AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
	    SET outRef."total-unapproved-recovery-request-amount" = CAST(CAST(damagedObjectsItem1Ref.totalUnapprovedRecoveryRequestsAmountPerDamagedObject AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
	    SET outRef."total-claim-reserve-amount"               = CAST(CAST(damagedObjectsItem1Ref.totalClaimReserveAmountPerDamagedObject AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);						

		--external-claim
		FOR externalClaimIDITRef AS inRef.relatedExternalClaims.Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)"external-claim" AS externalClaimRef NAME 'Item';
			SET externalClaimRef."external-claim-id"				  = externalClaimIDITRef.id;
			SET externalClaimRef."external-claim-system"			  = externalClaimIDITRef.externalSystem;						
			SET externalClaimRef."external-claimed-cover-internal-id" = externalClaimIDITRef.claimNumber;
	    END FOR;	    

        --travelers
		FOR listOfTravelersIDITRef AS inRef.listOfTravelers.Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)travelers AS travelersRef NAME 'Item';
		    SET travelersRef."functional-id" 		= listOfTravelersIDITRef.travelerCategory.id;
		    SET travelersRef."technical-id" 		= listOfTravelersIDITRef.id;
			SET travelersRef."personal-id"			= listOfTravelersIDITRef.travelerCategory.id;			
			SET travelersRef."category-description" = listOfTravelersIDITRef.travelerCategory.desc;
			SET travelersRef."first-name"			= listOfTravelersIDITRef.firstName;
			SET travelersRef."last-name"			= listOfTravelersIDITRef.lastName;
			IF(listOfTravelersIDITRef.youngerThan18 = 0) THEN
				SET travelersRef."younger-18-years" = FALSE;
			END IF;
			IF(listOfTravelersIDITRef.youngerThan18 = 1) THEN
				SET travelersRef."younger-18-years" = TRUE;
			END IF;
		END FOR;
		
		--Event block
		DECLARE eventRef,locationRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef AS eventRef TYPE Name NAME 'event';
		SET eventRef."functional-id"         = inRef.eventTypeId;				
		SET eventRef."primary-cause-of-loss" = inRef.claimCauseOfLossVO.desc;
		SET eventRef.type					 = inRef.eventType.desc;
		SET eventRef."ext-code"				 = inRef.eventType.extCode;
		SET eventRef.description			 = inRef.claimEventDescription;
		SET eventRef.date					 = SUBSTRING(inRef.claimEventDate BEFORE 'T');
		
		--Event.location block
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF eventRef AS locationRef TYPE Name NAME 'location';
		SET locationRef."functional-id" = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.locationType.id;
		SET locationRef."technical-id"	= inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.id;
		SET locationRef."type-id"	    = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.locationType.id;
		SET locationRef."type-desc"		= inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.locationType.desc;
		SET locationRef."street-name"   = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.addressIBO."streetName";
		SET locationRef."house-numer"   = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.addressIBO.houseNr;
		SET locationRef.city		    = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.addressIBO.cityName;
		SET locationRef."zip-code" 		= inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.addressIBO.zipCode;	
		SET locationRef.country		    = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.countryVO.desc;
		SET locationRef."country-code"  = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.addressIBO.country.extCode;		
		SET locationRef."pob-nr"	    = inRef.claimEventLocationIBO.addressIBO.pob;	
		
		--group-claim		
		DECLARE groupClaimRef,catastropheRef,locationCatastropheRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;		
		FOR claimGroupsRef AS inRef.claimGroups.Item[] DO 
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)"group-claim" AS groupClaimRef NAME 'Item';
			SET groupClaimRef.id 		   = claimGroupsRef.id;
			SET groupClaimRef."ext-number" = claimGroupsRef.claimGroupExternalNumber;
			SET groupClaimRef.description  = claimGroupsRef.claimGroupDescription;
			SET groupClaimRef."type-id"	   = claimGroupsRef.claimGroupType.id;
			SET groupClaimRef."type-desc"  = claimGroupsRef.claimGroupType.desc;
		END	FOR;
		
		--group-claim/Catastrophe
		DECLARE claimCatastropheRef ROW inRef.claimCatastrophe;
		IF EXISTS(claimCatastropheRef.*[]) THEN
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF groupClaimRef AS catastropheRef TYPE Name NAME 'catastrophe';
			SET catastropheRef.id 			= claimCatastropheRef.id;				
			SET catastropheRef."ext-number" = claimCatastropheRef.catastropheExternalNumber;
			SET catastropheRef.description  = claimCatastropheRef.catastropheDescription;
			SET catastropheRef."start-date" = SUBSTRING(claimCatastropheRef.startDate BEFORE 'T');
			SET catastropheRef."end-date"  	= SUBSTRING(claimCatastropheRef.endDate BEFORE 'T');
			SET catastropheRef."type-id"    = claimCatastropheRef.catastropheType.id;
			SET catastropheRef."type-desc"  = claimCatastropheRef.catastropheType.desc;
			FOR catastropheCountryRef AS claimCatastropheRef.catastropheListOfCountries.Item[] DO
				CREATE LASTCHILD OF catastropheRef.(JSON.Array)location AS locationCatastropheRef NAME 'Item';
				SET locationCatastropheRef."type-id"       = catastropheCountryRef.id;
				SET locationCatastropheRef."type-ext-code" = catastropheCountryRef.extCode;
				SET locationCatastropheRef."type-desc"     = catastropheCountryRef.desc;
			END FOR;	
		END IF;
		
		--claim-contacts 			
		FOR relatedClaimContactsRef AS inRef.relatedClaimContacts.Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)"claim-contacts" AS claimContactsRef NAME 'Item';
				SET claimContactsRef."functional-id" = relatedClaimContactsRef.contactExtNumber;
				SET claimContactsRef."technical-id"  = cast(relatedClaimContactsRef.id AS INT DEFAULT NULL);			
				SET claimContactsRef."personal-id" 	 = relatedClaimContactsRef.contactExtNumber;
				SET claimContactsRef."role-desc" 	 = relatedClaimContactsRef.claimContactRoleVO.desc; 
				SET claimContactsRef.role   		 = relatedClaimContactsRef.claimContactRoleVO.extCode;
				SET claimContactsRef.type   		 = relatedClaimContactsRef.claimContactRoleVO.id;
		END FOR; 
		
		--claimed-cover
		DECLARE claimedCoverRef,claimedDamagesRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data; 
		FOR claimantIBOsRef AS inRef.claimantIBOs.Item[] DO
			FOR damagedObjectsRef AS claimantIBOsRef.damagedObjects.Item[] DO
				DECLARE damagedObjectsRefUnique ROW;
				
				 FOR damagesRef AS damagedObjectsRef.damages.Item[] DO
					
					IF NOT EXISTS(damagesRef.damageCover[]) THEN
						SET damagedObjectsRefUnique.result[] = SELECT * FROM damagedObjectsRef.damages.Item[] AS t WHERE NOT EXISTS(t.damageCover[]);
					ELSE
						SET damagedObjectsRefUnique.result[] = SELECT * FROM damagedObjectsRef.damages.Item[] AS t WHERE t.damageCover.id = damagesRef.damageCover.id;
					END IF;
					IF NOT EXISTS(Environment.Variables.ClaimedCoverCreated.{cast(COALESCE(damagedObjectsRefUnique.result.damageCover.id,'11111') AS CHAR)}[]) THEN
						SET Environment.Variables.ClaimedCoverCreated.{cast(COALESCE(damagedObjectsRefUnique.result.damageCover.id,'11111') AS CHAR)} = TRUE;
						CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)"claimed-cover" AS claimedCoverRef NAME 'Item';
						FOR damagesRef AS damagedObjectsRefUnique.result[] DO
					   	
					   	   	SET claimedCoverRef."functional-id"		= damagesRef.damageCover.id;
					   	   	SET claimedCoverRef."technical-id"		= damagedObjectsRef.id;
					   	   	SET claimedCoverRef.id 					= damagesRef.damageCover.id;
					   		SET claimedCoverRef."cover-description" = damagesRef.damageCover.desc;				   		
					   		SET claimedCoverRef."update-version"   	= damagedObjectsRef.updateVersion;
							SET claimedCoverRef.status   			= damagedObjectsRef.damagedObjectStatus.desc;
							SET claimedCoverRef."status-reason"     = damagedObjectsRef.damagedObjectStatusReason.desc;
							SET claimedCoverRef."state-id"   	    = damagedObjectsRef.damagedObjectState.id;
							SET claimedCoverRef."state-reason"	    = damagedObjectsRef.damagedObjectState.desc;
							SET claimedCoverRef."cover-deductible"  = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.claimedCoverDeductible AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);	
							SET claimedCoverRef."insured-amount"	= CAST(CAST(damagesRef.claimedCoverAggregatedInsuranceAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);							
														
					   		--claimed-cover.claimed-damages	
					   		DECLARE deductibleLine DECIMAL 0;
					   		DECLARE counterBooking DECIMAL 0;
					   		DECLARE totalApprovedClaimPaymentGrossAmount DECIMAL damagesRef.totalApprovedClaimPaymentsAmountPerDamage;
					   		FOR claimTransactionIVORef AS damagesRef.claimTransactionIVO.Item[] DO
					   			FOR claimTransactionLineIVOsRef AS claimTransactionIVORef.claimTransactionLineIVOs.Item[] DO
					   				IF claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.extCode = '500008' THEN
					   					SET deductibleLine = ABS(claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.amount);
					   				ELSEIF claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.extCode = '500110' THEN
					   					SET counterBooking = ABS(claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.amount);
					   				END IF;
					   			END FOR; 
					   		END FOR;
					   		IF deductibleLine > 0 AND counterBooking > 0 THEN
					   			SET totalApprovedClaimPaymentGrossAmount = totalApprovedClaimPaymentGrossAmount - deductibleLine + counterBooking;
					   		ELSEIF deductibleLine > 0 THEN
					   			SET totalApprovedClaimPaymentGrossAmount = totalApprovedClaimPaymentGrossAmount + deductibleLine;
					   		END IF;
					   		
					   		CREATE FIELD claimedCoverRef."claimed-damages" AS claimedDamagesRef IDENTITY(JSON.Array)"claimed-damages";							
							CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimedCoverRef."claimed-damages" AS claimedDamagesRef NAME 'Item';					  
							SET claimedDamagesRef."functional-id"		                     = damagesRef.damageCode.id;
							SET claimedDamagesRef."technical-id"		                     = damagesRef.id; 
					   	    SET claimedDamagesRef.id   					                     = damagesRef.id;
							SET claimedDamagesRef.extCode   			                     = damagesRef.damageCode.extCode;
							SET claimedDamagesRef.description			                     = damagesRef.damageCode.desc;
							SET claimedDamagesRef."status-id"			                     = damagesRef.damageStatus.id;
							SET claimedDamagesRef."status-desc"			                     = damagesRef.damageStatus.desc;
							SET claimedDamagesRef."state-id"			                     = damagesRef.damageState.id;
							SET claimedDamagesRef."state-desc"			                     = damagesRef.damageState.desc;							
							SET claimedDamagesRef."update-version"		                     = damagesRef.updateVersion;
							SET claimedDamagesRef."insured-amount"		                     = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.claimedCoverInsuranceAmountPerEvent AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							SET claimedDamagesRef."total-approved-claim-payment-amount"      = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.totalApprovedClaimPaymentsAmountPerDamage AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							SET claimedDamagesRef."total-unapproved-claim-payment-amount"    = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.totalUnapprovedClaimPaymentsAmountPerDamage AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							SET claimedDamagesRef."total-approved-recovery-request-amount"   = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.totalApprovedRecoveryRequestsAmountPerDamage AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							SET claimedDamagesRef."total-unapproved-recovery-request-amount" = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.totalUnapprovedRecoveryRequestsAmountPerDamage AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							SET claimedDamagesRef."total-approved-claim-payment-gross-amount"= CAST(CAST(totalApprovedClaimPaymentGrossAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							
							DECLARE damagedObjectsDetailsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;	
							CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimedDamagesRef AS damagedObjectsDetailsRef NAME 'damaged-objects-details';						 
							--claimed-cover.claimed-damages.damaged-objects-details
							IF EXISTS(damagedObjectsRef.damagedItems.Item[]) THEN						   						  
							    FOR damagedItemsIDITRef AS damagedObjectsRef.damagedItems.Item[] DO
							        CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsDetailsRef.(JSON.Array)"damage-items" AS damagedItemsRef NAME 'Item';
							   		SET damagedItemsRef."functional-id"  = damagedItemsIDITRef.itemType.id;
							   		SET damagedItemsRef."technical-id"   = damagedItemsIDITRef.id;
							   		SET damagedItemsRef.id				 = damagedItemsIDITRef.itemType.id;
									SET damagedItemsRef.description		 = damagedItemsIDITRef.itemType.desc;
									SET damagedItemsRef.remarks			 = damagedItemsIDITRef.itemType.remarks;
									SET damagedItemsRef."total-loss"     = damagedItemsIDITRef.totalLoss;
									SET damagedItemsRef."purchase-value" = CAST(CAST(damagedItemsIDITRef.purchaseValue AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);		
									SET damagedItemsRef."ext-code"		 = damagedItemsIDITRef.itemType.extCode;
									SET damagedItemsRef."is-active"		 = damagedItemsIDITRef.isActive;																						   		
							   		MOVE damagedItemsIDITRef NEXTSIBLING;
							    END FOR;						   		
							END IF;
						 	CASE productCode
							 	WHEN '103' THEN 
							 		 -- call travel-infos();
                                     DECLARE travelInfosRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
                                     CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsDetailsRef AS travelInfosRef NAME 'travel-infos';-- value null;
                                     DECLARE travelDatesIDITRef REFERENCE TO inRef.travelDates;
                                     SET travelInfosRef."technical-id"                              = travelDatesIDITRef.id;
                                     SET travelInfosRef."travel-booking-date"                        = SUBSTRING(travelDatesIDITRef.travelBookingDate BEFORE 'T');
                                     SET travelInfosRef."planned-travel-departure-date"                = SUBSTRING(travelDatesIDITRef.travelDepartureDate BEFORE 'T');
                                     SET travelInfosRef."planned-travel-return-date"                = SUBSTRING(travelDatesIDITRef.travelReturnDate BEFORE 'T');
                                     SET travelInfosRef."travel-duration"                            = travelDatesIDITRef.tripDuration;
                                     SET travelInfosRef."impediment-date"                            = SUBSTRING(travelDatesIDITRef.dateOfImpediment BEFORE 'T');
                                     SET travelInfosRef."impediment-departure-dates-interval"        = travelDatesIDITRef.departureDateImpedimentDateInterval;
                                     SET travelInfosRef."event-date"                                = SUBSTRING(inRef.claimEventDate BEFORE 'T');
                                     SET travelInfosRef."event-departure-dates-interval"            = travelDatesIDITRef.departureDateEventDateInterval;
                                     
							 		-- call cdw();
							 		FOR accidentCircumstancesQuestionnaireRef AS damagedObjectsRef.accidentCircumstancesQuestionnaire.Item[] DO
								 		DECLARE questionnaireLineIBOs ROW;
								 		SET questionnaireLineIBOs.result[] =  SELECT * 
								 		                                      FROM accidentCircumstancesQuestionnaireRef.questionnaireLineIBOs.Item[] AS questionnaireLineIBOs 
								 		                                      WHERE questionnaireLineIBOs.questionnaireLineVO.id = 1000005 OR
								 		                                            questionnaireLineIBOs.questionnaireLineVO.id = 1000006 OR
								 		                                            questionnaireLineIBOs.questionnaireLineVO.id = 1000008;
								 		DECLARE cdwRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot;
								 		FOR questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef AS questionnaireLineIBOs.result[] DO
								 			IF NOT EXISTS(damagedObjectsDetailsRef.CDW[]) THEN
								 				CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsDetailsRef AS cdwRef NAME 'CDW';
								 			END IF;
								 			SET cdwRef."technical-id" = questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.id;
							 				IF (questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.questionnaireLineVO.id = '1000005') THEN
						                		SET cdwRef."vehicle-type-one"	= questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.answerIdValue;
							 				END IF;
							 				IF (questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.questionnaireLineVO.id = '1000006') THEN
						                		SET cdwRef."damage-type-one"	= questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.answerIdValue;
							 				END IF;
							 				IF (questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.questionnaireLineVO.id = '1000008') THEN
						                		SET cdwRef."vehicle-type-two"	= questionnaireLineIBOsIditRef.answerIdValue;					 				
							 				END IF;
								 		END FOR;
							 		END FOR;
							 		-- call luggage();
									 --DECLARE luggageRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot;
									 --CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsDetailsRef AS luggageRef NAME 'luggage' value null;
							 	WHEN '105' THEN 
							 		-- call bike();
							 		DECLARE bikeRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
							 		FOR bikeIDITRef AS inRef.insuredBikeDetails.Item[] DO
								 		CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsDetailsRef.(JSON.Array)bike AS bikeRef NAME 'Item';
								 		SET bikeRef.type				= bikeIDITRef.bikeType.desc;
								 		SET bikeRef."type-ext-code"		= bikeIDITRef.bikeType.extCode;
								 		SET bikeRef.brand				= bikeIDITRef.bikeBrand.desc;
								 		SET bikeRef.color				= bikeIDITRef.bikeColor.extCode;
								 		SET bikeRef."color-description"	= bikeIDITRef.bikeColor.desc;
								 		SET bikeRef.frame				= bikeIDITRef.frame;	
								 		SET bikeRef."25km-more"			= bikeIDITRef.reachesOver25KMperHour;	
								 		SET bikeRef.plate				= bikeIDITRef.plate;
								 		SET bikeRef."purchase-date"		= SUBSTRING(bikeIDITRef.purchaseDate BEFORE 'T');	
								 		SET bikeRef."first-reg-date"	= SUBSTRING(bikeIDITRef.firstRegistrationDate BEFORE 'T');	
								 		SET bikeRef."purchase-value"	= CAST(CAST(bikeIDITRef.bikeValue AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
							 		END FOR;						 									 									 								 		
							 	WHEN '102' THEN 
							 		-- call camping-material();	
									 --DECLARE campingMaterialRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot;
									 --CREATE LASTCHILD OF damagedObjectsDetailsRef AS campingMaterialRef NAME 'camping-material' value null;
							 		
							 	END CASE;
							--reserve
							DECLARE reserveRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
							DECLARE reimbursementRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
							DECLARE recoveryRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
							DECLARE reasonRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
							FOR reserveIVORef AS damagesRef.reserveIVO.Item[] DO
								--NIPO-6178, suite à implementation changer la langue, le mapping avec description ne marche plus,  il faut remplacer par id.
								IF reserveIVORef.reserveTypeVO.id = 10001  THEN	-- 10001 'PAYMENT', Si le reserveTypeVO est payment, il faut créer le block reserve et reimbursement								
									CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimedDamagesRef.(JSON.Array)reserve AS reserveRef NAME 'Item';
									SET reserveRef."total-amount" = CAST(CAST(reserveIVORef.totalReserveAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
									SET reserveRef.reason		  = reserveIVORef.claimReserveReasonVO.desc;
									SET reserveRef.type			  = reserveIVORef.reserveTypeVO.desc;
									SET reserveRef.currency		  = reserveIVORef.currencyVO.extCode;
									DECLARE reservePaymentDetailsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot;
									CREATE LASTCHILD OF reserveRef.(JSON.Array)"payment-details" AS reservePaymentDetailsRef NAME 'Item';
									FOR reserveLineIVOsRef AS reserveIVORef.reserveLineIVOs.Item[] DO
										SET reservePaymentDetailsRef.id					 = reserveLineIVOsRef.id;
										SET reservePaymentDetailsRef."invoice-line-id"	 = reserveLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.id;
										SET reservePaymentDetailsRef."invoice-line-desc" = reserveLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.desc;
										SET reservePaymentDetailsRef.amount				 = CAST(CAST(reserveLineIVOsRef.amount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
										SET reservePaymentDetailsRef."type-ext-code" 	 = reserveLineIVOsRef.creditDebitVO.extCode;
										SET reservePaymentDetailsRef."prev-amount"		 = CAST(CAST(reserveLineIVOsRef.prevAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
									END FOR;
								END IF;	
							END FOR;							
							DECLARE rembourserPaymentDetailsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
							DECLARE recoveryPaymentDetailsRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;							
							FOR claimTransactionIVORef AS damagesRef.claimTransactionIVO.Item[] DO
							    --reimbursement
							    IF  claimTransactionIVORef.paymentTypeVO.id = 10001  THEN -- 10001 'PAYMENT'																											
									CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimedDamagesRef.(JSON.Array)reimbursement AS reimbursementRef NAME 'Item';
									SET reimbursementRef."estimated-amount" 			= CAST(damagesRef.estimatedDamageAmount AS DECIMAL);
									    IF claimTransactionIVORef.statusVO.id = 2  THEN  -- 2 'APPROVED'
											SET reimbursementRef."approved-amount"			= CAST(CAST(claimTransactionIVORef.totalTransactionAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
									    END IF;
									    IF  claimTransactionIVORef.statusVO.id = 1 --'PENDING'
									    	OR claimTransactionIVORef.statusVO.id = 10001 -- 'PAYMENT TO APPROVE'
									    	OR claimTransactionIVORef.statusVO.id = 3 --'REJECTED'
									    	OR claimTransactionIVORef.statusVO.id = 10022 -- 'PENDING APPROVAL' 
									    	OR claimTransactionIVORef.statusVO.id = 1000006 -- 'Ex-gratia payment to approve'
									   	THEN
											SET reimbursementRef."unapproved-amount"		= CAST(CAST(claimTransactionIVORef.totalTransactionAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
									    END IF;
										SET reimbursementRef.currency						= claimTransactionIVORef.currencyVO.extCode;
									--rembourser payment-detail																						
									FOR claimTransactionLineIVOsRef AS claimTransactionIVORef.claimTransactionLineIVOs.Item[] DO	
										CREATE LASTCHILD OF reimbursementRef.(JSON.Array)"payment-details" AS rembourserPaymentDetailsRef NAME 'Item';																																							        								
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef.id					= claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.id;
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef."invoice-line-id"	= claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.id;
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef."invoice-line-desc"	= claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.desc;
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef.amount				= CAST(CAST(claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.amount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef."value-date"		= SUBSTRING(claimTransactionIVORef.valueDate BEFORE 'T');
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef."type-ext-code" 	= claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.creditDebitVO.extCode;
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef."payment-method"	= claimTransactionIVORef.claimPaymentMethodVO.extCode;
										SET rembourserPaymentDetailsRef."iban-number"		= claimTransactionIVORef.ibanNumber;
									END FOR;
							    END IF;																				
								--recovery
								IF  claimTransactionIVORef.paymentTypeVO.id = 10002 THEN  --10002 'RECOVERY'
									CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimedDamagesRef.(JSON.Array)recovery AS recoveryRef NAME 'Item';
									SET recoveryRef."total-amount" 			        = CAST(CAST(claimTransactionIVORef.totalTransactionAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
									FOR claimTransactionLineIVOsRef AS claimTransactionIVORef.claimTransactionLineIVOs.Item[] DO
										CREATE LASTCHILD OF recoveryRef.(JSON.Array)reason AS reasonRef NAME 'Item';									
										SET reasonRef							    = claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.creditDebitVO.extCode;
									END FOR;
									SET recoveryRef.type							= claimTransactionIVORef.paymentTypeVO.desc;	
									SET recoveryRef.currency						= claimTransactionIVORef.currencyVO.extCode;
								-- recovery payment-detail																									
									FOR claimTransactionLineIVOsRef AS claimTransactionIVORef.claimTransactionLineIVOs.Item[] DO	
										CREATE LASTCHILD OF recoveryRef.(JSON.Array)"payment-details" AS recoveryPaymentDetailsRef NAME 'Item';																			        								
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef.id				  = claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.id;
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef."invoice-line-id"	  = claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.id;
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef."invoice-line-desc" = claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.invoiceLineVO.desc;
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef.amount			  = CAST(CAST(claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.amount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef."type-ext-code" 	  = claimTransactionLineIVOsRef.creditDebitVO.extCode;
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef."payment-method"	  = claimTransactionIVORef.claimPaymentMethodVO.extCode;
										SET recoveryPaymentDetailsRef."iban-number"		  = claimTransactionIVORef.ibanNumber;
									END FOR;									
								END IF;
							END FOR;		
						END FOR;
					   
					END IF;
					--NIPO-5484
					SET claimedCoverRef."cover-deductible" = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.claimedCoverDeductible AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
					SET claimedCoverRef."insured-amount"   = CAST(CAST(damagesRef.claimedCoverAggregatedInsuranceAmount AS FLOAT) AS CHARACTER FORMAT pattern);
				 END FOR;
			END FOR;
		END FOR;
		
		--claim-service-providers
		DECLARE serviceProviderRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		FOR serviceProvidersIDITRef AS inRef.serviceProviders.Item[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)"claim-service-providers" AS serviceProviderRef NAME 'Item';
			SET serviceProviderRef."personal-id" = serviceProvidersIDITRef.contactExtNumber;
			SET serviceProviderRef."type-id"	 = serviceProvidersIDITRef.serviceProviderTypeVO.id;						
			SET serviceProviderRef."ext-code" 	 = serviceProvidersIDITRef.serviceProviderTypeVO.extCode;
			SET serviceProviderRef.description   = serviceProvidersIDITRef.serviceProviderTypeVO.desc;
		END FOR;
	   
	    RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/mappingResponseIditGetClaimsByPersonalId.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE mappingResponseIditGetClaimsByPersonalId
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data;
		DECLARE inRef REFERENCE TO InputRoot.JSON.Data.Item;
		DECLARE outRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;	
	
		--claims
		FOR claimIditRef AS inRef.claimInListIBO.Item[] DO
			DECLARE claimRef,eventRef,locationRef,contactRef REFERENCE TO OutputRoot.JSON.Data;	
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF outRef.(JSON.Array)claims AS claimRef NAME 'Item';
			SET claimRef."technical-id"      = claimIditRef.id;
			SET claimRef."claim-number"		 = claimIditRef."claimNr";
			SET claimRef."status"			 = claimIditRef.claimStatus.id;						
			SET claimRef."status-desc"		 = claimIditRef.claimStatus.desc;
			SET claimRef."update-version"	 = claimIditRef.updateVersion;
			SET claimRef."notification-date" = claimIditRef.claimSubmissionDate;
			SET claimRef."policy-number"	 = claimIditRef.policyNr;
    	    --event block
    	    CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimRef AS eventRef NAME 'event';
    	    SET eventRef.description		 = claimIditRef.claimEventDescription;
    	    SET eventRef.date				 = claimIditRef.claimDate;
    	    --location block
    	    CREATE LASTCHILD OF eventRef AS locationRef NAME 'location';
    	    --SET locationRef."technical-id"					= claimIditRef.claimEventLocationVO.id; TODO
    	    SET locationRef."functional-id"	 = claimIditRef.claimEventLocationVO.claimLocationTypeVO.id;  
    	    SET locationRef."type-id"		 = claimIditRef.claimEventLocationVO.claimLocationTypeVO.id;    	  
            SET locationRef."type-desc"		 = claimIditRef.claimEventLocationVO.claimLocationTypeVO.desc;
            SET locationRef.country			 = claimIditRef.claimEventLocationVO.countryVO.desc;
            SET locationRef."country-code"	 = claimIditRef.claimEventLocationVO.countryVO.extCode;
            --claim-contacts
            --Extraire le personal-id dans policy number, par exemple : PO105597106-10300/00
            DECLARE personalId CHAR  
			CASE
			 	WHEN (STARTSWITH(UPPER(claimIditRef.policyNr),'PO')) THEN SUBSTRING(SUBSTRING(UPPER(claimIditRef.policyNr) BEFORE '-')FROM 3 FOR 9)
			 	ELSE Environment.Variables.claim."claim-contacts"."personal-id"						
			END;
            CREATE LASTCHILD OF claimRef AS contactRef NAME 'claim-contacts';  
            SET contactRef."personal-id" 					= personalId;  
		END FOR; 
	   
	   RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareCallGetContact
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader.Accept = '*/*';
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables.XCorrelationID;
		DECLARE URL CHARACTER COALESCE(getPolicyProperty('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
		DECLARE PATH CHARACTER COALESCE(getPolicyProperty('{insurance-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndPointsPolicy','path-insurance-policies-integration-technical-idit-contact-get-contact-tcs'), '');

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URL || PATH || '/' || Environment.Variables.ibanHolderPersonalReference;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'GET';
		
		--Logs
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05614';
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareCallPutContact
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader.Accept = '*/*';
		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables.XCorrelationID;
		DECLARE URL CHARACTER COALESCE(getPolicyProperty('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
		DECLARE PATH CHARACTER COALESCE(getPolicyProperty('{insurance-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndPointsPolicy','path-insurance-policies-integration-technical-idit-contact-get-contact-tcs'), '');

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = URL || PATH || '/' || Environment.Variables.ibanHolderPersonalReference;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'PUT';
		
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
		
		--Logs
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05614';
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/RetryOrSendToBO.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE RetryOrSendToBO
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		--Logs
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05625';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = 'insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit';
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = UNKNOWN;
	    SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.Error.httpMessage || '. ' || Environment.Variables.Error.moreInformation, 'Internal Server Error');
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = COALESCE(Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode, '500');	
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1' DELETE NONE;
		
		IF Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode IS NULL OR STARTSWITH(Environment.Variables.Error.httpCode, '4') THEN --internal or client error: message sent to BO
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
			SET Environment.Variables.IDITerror = TRUE;  
			
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		    SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;
		    SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined = Environment.Variables.userDefined;
		        
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2';
		ELSE
			IF Environment.Variables.retryCount IS NULL THEN --first retry
				SET Environment.Variables.userDefined.retryCount = 1;
				SET Environment.Variables.userDefined.timestamp = CURRENT_GMTTIMESTAMP;
		        SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;	  
		        
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;	
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined = Environment.Variables.userDefined;	
		              
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out';
			ELSEIF Environment.Variables.retryCount = 3 THEN --already retried 3 times: message sent to BO
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
				SET Environment.Variables.IDITerror = TRUE;
				
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined = Environment.Variables.userDefined;
		        
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2';
			ELSE                                             --retry again
				SET Environment.Variables.userDefined.retryCount = CAST((CAST(Environment.Variables.retryCount AS INTEGER) + 1) AS CHARACTER);
				SET Environment.Variables.userDefined.timestamp = Environment.Variables.timestamp;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
				
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction;
		        SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined = Environment.Variables.userDefined;
		        
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out';		
			END IF;		
		END IF;			
		
		RETURN FALSE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit/apps/insurance_claims_integration_technical_idit/SetEnvironment.esql

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE SetEnvironment
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;

		SET Environment.Variables.retryCount = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined.retryCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.timestamp = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined.timestamp;

		SET Environment.Variables.policyNumber = InputRoot.JSON.Data."policy-number";
		DECLARE email ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'e-mail');
		SET Environment.Variables.email = email."value-code";
		DECLARE language ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'language');
		SET Environment.Variables.language = language."value-code";
		DECLARE informantPersonalReference ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data."claim-contacts".Item[] AS A WHERE A.role = 'informant');
		SET Environment.Variables.informantPersonalReference = informantPersonalReference."personal-id";
		DECLARE policyHolderPersonalReference ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data."claim-contacts".Item[] AS A WHERE A.role = 'polHold');
		SET Environment.Variables.policyHolderPersonalReference = policyHolderPersonalReference."personal-id";
		DECLARE ibanHolderPersonalReference ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'iban-holder');
		SET Environment.Variables.ibanHolderPersonalReference = ibanHolderPersonalReference."value-code";
		DECLARE numberOfDocuments ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'number-of-documents');
		SET Environment.Variables.numberOfDocuments = numberOfDocuments."value-code";
		DECLARE documentListGUUID ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'document-list-guuid');
		SET Environment.Variables.documentListGUUID = documentListGUUID."value-code";
		SET Environment.Variables.documentList[] = SELECT * FROM documentListGUUID."sub-attributes".Item[];
		DECLARE keyValuesOfOnlineForm ROW THE(SELECT A FROM InputRoot.JSON.Data.attributes.Item[] AS A WHERE A.type = 'key-values-of-online-form');
		SET Environment.Variables.keyValuesOfOnlineForm = keyValuesOfOnlineForm."value-code";

		SET Environment.Variables.policy.apicHost = COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{common-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndpointsPolicy','ace-host-insurance'), '');
		SET Environment.Variables.policy.claimPost = COALESCE(gestionPolicy('{insurance-integration-policies-endpoints}:AceEndPointsPolicy','path-insurance-claims-integration-technical-idit-loss-event-tcs'), '');

		--Set variables in Env to trace in SUP Log
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.transaction = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers";

		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.parentTransactionId;
		SET Environment.Variables.XCorrelationID = Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.globalTransactionId;
		SET Environment.Variables.In.XCallerCode = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code";

		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05625';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';

	    SET Environment.Variables.Info.RETRY_COUNT = Environment.Variables.retryCount;


		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/apps/insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/common/errorHandler_Compute.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE errorHandler_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE responseAsJSON ROW; 
		DECLARE code, message, details CHARACTER;
		DECLARE enc INTEGER COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.Encoding, 546);
		DECLARE charSet INTEGER COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);

		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.ContentType = 'application/json; charset=utf-8';
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET Environment.Variables.error = TRUE;

		CASE
			WHEN CARDINALITY(InputExceptionList.*[]) > 0
			THEN
				DECLARE number_out INTEGER;
				DECLARE text_out CHARACTER;
				DECLARE stacktrace_out CHARACTER;

				SET code = '500';

				CALL GetException(InputExceptionList, number_out, text_out, stacktrace_out);

				IF COALESCE(text_out, '') <> ''
				THEN
					SET message = 'ACETECHERR001: ' || COALESCE(CAST(number_out AS CHARACTER), '0000') || ' - ' || text_out;
				ELSE
					SET message = 'ACETECHERR001: ' || code;
				END IF;

				IF COALESCE(stacktrace_out, '') <> ''
				THEN
					SET details = stacktrace_out;
				ELSE
					SET details = 'Internal Server Error.';
				END IF;
			WHEN CARDINALITY(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader[]) > 0
			THEN
				DECLARE responseAsBlob BLOB;
				IF EXISTS(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB[])
				THEN
					SET responseAsBlob  = InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB;
				ELSEIF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data[])
				THEN
					SET responseAsBlob = ASBITSTREAM(InputRoot.JSON.Data CCSID charSet Encoding enc);
				END IF;

				DECLARE firstByte BLOB SUBSTRING(responseAsBlob FROM 1 FOR 1);

				-- { = 0x7B
				IF firstByte = X'7B'
				THEN
					CREATE LASTCHILD OF responseAsJSON DOMAIN('JSON') PARSE(responseAsBlob);
				END IF;

				IF EXISTS(responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.*[]) AND COALESCE(responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.httpCode, '') <> ''
					AND COALESCE(responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.httpMessage, '') <> '' AND COALESCE(responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.moreInformation, '') <> ''
				THEN
					SET code = responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.httpCode;
					SET message = responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.httpMessage;
					SET details = responseAsJSON.JSON.Data.moreInformation;
				ELSE
					SET code = CAST(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code" AS CHARACTER);
					SET message = 'Error when requesting backend API ' || COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Line", '.');
					SET details = CAST(responseAsBlob AS CHARACTER CCSID charSet Encoding enc);
				END IF;
			ELSE
				SET code = '500';
				SET message = 'unknown error';
				SET details = 'unknown error';
		END CASE;

		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP AS CHAR FORMAT 'I');
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = code;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = message;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = details;

		--ReplyStatusCode
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = code;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
	
	CREATE FUNCTION GetException(IN InputTree reference, OUT number_out integer, OUT text_out char, OUT stacktrace_out char)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE stacktrace CHARACTER '';
		DECLARE start REFERENCE TO InputExceptionList.*[1];

		WHILE LASTMOVE(start)
		DO
			IF start.Number IS NOT NULL
			THEN
				SET number_out = start.Number;
				SET text_out = start.Text;
				SET stacktrace = stacktrace || ':' || CAST(start.Number as CHARACTER) || ':' || start.Text;

				DECLARE insertRef REFERENCE TO start;
				MOVE insertRef FIRSTCHILD TYPE Name NAME 'Insert';
				WHILE LASTMOVE(insertRef)
				DO
					SET stacktrace = stacktrace || ':' || insertRef.Text;
					MOVE insertRef NEXTSIBLING REPEAT TYPE NAME;
				END WHILE;
				SET stacktrace = stacktrace || ';';
			END IF;
			MOVE start LASTCHILD;
		END WHILe;

		SET stacktrace_out = stacktrace;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/apps/insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/common/prepareRequest_Compute.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common
PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE prepareRequest_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE endpointUrl CHARACTER;

		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		IF RequiresClientCredentials
		THEN
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Ibm-Client-Id" = gestionPolicy('{AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicies}:AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicy','client_id');
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-IBM-Client-Secret" = gestionPolicy('{AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicies}:AceAppEsbCredentialsPolicy','client_secret');
		END IF;

		IF RequiresToken
		THEN
			SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader.Authorization = 'Bearer ' || Environment.Variables.access_token;
		END IF;

		SET OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';

		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data[])
		THEN
			SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data = InputRoot.JSON.Data;
		END IF;

		-- SET endpoint url and method
		SET endpointUrl = Shlib_PoliciesReader.gestionPolicy(HostnamePolicy, Hostname);
		IF isNotNone(BasePath)
		THEN
			SET endpointUrl = endpointUrl || gestionPolicy(Policy, BasePath);
		END IF;
		SET endpointUrl = endpointUrl || gestionPolicy(Policy, OperationPath);

		CALL ReplacePathParams(endpointUrl, InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters);
		CALL AddHttpHeaderPathParams();
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = endpointUrl;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = Environment.Variables.request.method;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.QueryString = InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Input.QueryString;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
	
	CREATE FUNCTION isNotNone(IN param CHARACTER) RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		IF param <> '' AND param <> none
		THEN
			RETURN TRUE;
		ELSE
			RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
	END;
 
	
	CREATE PROCEDURE ReplacePathParams(INOUT uri CHARACTER, IN params REFERENCE) 
	BEGIN
	    DECLARE param REFERENCE TO params.*[1];

	    WHILE LASTMOVE(param) AND CONTAINS(uri, '{') AND CONTAINS(uri, '}')
	    DO
	        DECLARE paramName  CHARACTER FIELDNAME(param);
	        DECLARE paramValue CHARACTER CAST(param AS CHARACTER);

	        SET uri = REPLACE(uri, '{' || paramName || '}', paramValue);

	        MOVE param NEXTSIBLING;
	    END WHILE;
	END;
	CREATE PROCEDURE AddHttpHeaderPathParams() 
	BEGIN
	   	    for ParamIn as InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters.*[] do
		    	DECLARE HeaderName CHAR lower(FIELDNAME(ParamIn));
		    	DECLARE HeaderValue CHAR FIELDVALUE(ParamIn);
		    	check:BEGIN	
			    	for HeaderIn as InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader.*[] do
			    		if lower(FIELDNAME(ParamIn)) = lower(FIELDNAME(HeaderIn)) then
			    			set OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader.{FIELDNAME(ParamIn)} = FIELDVALUE(ParamIn);
			    			leave check;	
			    		end if;
			    	end for;
		    	end ;
	    end for;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/apps/insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/common/prepareResponse_Compute.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE prepareResponse_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB[])
		THEN
			SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		ELSEIF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data.*[])
		THEN
			SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		ELSE
			SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
			CREATE FIELD OutputRoot.JSON.Data IDENTITY (JSON.Object)Data;
		END IF;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 200;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

BROKER SCHEMA common

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE restoreRequest_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		IF COALESCE(Environment.Variables.request.httpInputHeader, '') <> ''
		THEN
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN ('HTTPInputHeader') PARSE(Environment.Variables.request.httpInputHeader);
		END IF;
		IF COALESCE(Environment.Variables.request.payload, '') <> ''
		THEN
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN('JSON') PARSE(Environment.Variables.request.payload);
		END IF;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters = Environment.Variables.request.parameters;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Input.QueryString = Environment.Variables.request.queryString;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

BROKER SCHEMA common

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE storeRequest_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE charSet INTEGER COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);

		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;

		CREATE FIELD Environment.Variables.message;

		SET Environment.Variables.request.httpInputHeader = ASBITSTREAM(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader CCSID charSet);

		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data[])
		THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.request.payload = ASBITSTREAM(InputRoot.JSON.Data CCSID charSet);
		END IF;
		SET Environment.Variables.request.parameters  = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters;
		SET Environment.Variables.request.queryString = InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Input.QueryString;
		set Environment.Variables.request.method = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Method;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/apps/insurance-integration-policies-shlib-passthrough-idit/common/token/Functions.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common.token

CREATE FUNCTION CheckTokenExpired() RETURNS BOOLEAN BEGIN	

	IF EXISTS(TOKENS.IDIT[])
	THEN
		IF (COALESCE(TOKENS.IDIT.access_token,'') = '' OR COALESCE(TOKENS.IDIT.expires_in,'') = '')
		THEN
			-- no existing valid token
			RETURN TRUE;
		END IF;

		DECLARE expiresIn INTEGER CAST(TOKENS.IDIT.expires_in AS INTEGER); 	-- in seconds
		DECLARE expiredAt TIMESTAMP TOKENS.IDIT.issued_at + CAST(expiresIn AS INTERVAL SECOND);

		IF expiredAt < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
		THEN
			-- token has expired
			RETURN TRUE;
		END IF;
		-- token is still valid
		RETURN FALSE;
	END IF;
	RETURN TRUE;
END;

CREATE FUNCTION GetToken() RETURNS CHARACTER 
BEGIN
	RETURN TOKENS.IDIT.access_token;
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE DeleteToken()
BEGIN
	DELETE FIELD TOKENS.IDIT;
END;

BROKER SCHEMA common.token
PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE PrepareRequest_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE endpointUrl CHARACTER;

		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		SET endpointUrl = gestionPolicy(TokenHostnamePolicy, TokenHostname);
		SET endpointUrl = endpointUrl || gestionPolicy(TokenUriPolicy, TokenUri);

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL = endpointUrl;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method = 'GET';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

BROKER SCHEMA common.token

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE storeToken_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET TOKENS.IDIT.access_token = InputRoot.JSON.Data.IditToken;
		SET TOKENS.IDIT.expires_in = InputRoot.JSON.Data.ExpiresIn;
		SET TOKENS.IDIT.issued_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; -- override existing response attribute
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/apps/common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls/callable_call.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_Failure
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			IF NOT EXISTS(OutputRoot.Properties[])
			THEN
				CREATE FIRSTCHILD of OutputRoot domain 'Properties' NAME 'Properties';
			END IF;
			IF Environment.Variables.SetContext IS UNKNOWN AND Environment.Variables.SetContextCallable IS UNKNOWN THEN
				SET Environment.Variables = Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.Environment.Variables;
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment = Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.LocalEnvironment;
			ELSE
				SET Environment = InputLocalEnvironment.Environment;
			END IF;
		END IF;
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable response Failure  (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName|| ')';
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;

		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'FAIL: Callable flow request failure exeption (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName|| ')';

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_async_call_Failure_Request
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-message" = messageText;

		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable Flow request Failure (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName || ')';
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;

        SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'FAIL: Callable flow request failure exeption (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName|| ')';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_Out
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			SET Environment = InputLocalEnvironment.Environment;
		END IF;		
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable response from ' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUCCESS: Callable request (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_async_call_SetLogMonitoring
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall NOT IN ('async','') THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please use ''sync'' or ''async'' for type-call parameter');
		END IF;
		IF not(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContext,false)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;
		IF cast(COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.requestTimeout,-1) AS INT DEFAULT -1) = -1 THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please provide requestTimeout in LocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.requestTimeout');
		END IF;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Source-System" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE	
		
		-- set infos variables
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		DELETE FIELD variablesRef.Info;		
		SET variablesRef.Info.targetApplication = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetApplication;
		SET variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetEndpointName;
		SET variablesRef.Info."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		
		-- set contextual variables
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context.targetApplication = variablesRef.Info.targetApplication;
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context.targetEndpointName = variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageInfo = 'INFO: SEND ' || Environment.Variables.TypeCall || ' request to callable ' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageSup = 'SUP: SEND ' || Environment.Variables.TypeCall || ' request to callable ' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;

		SET Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.Environment.Variables = Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.LocalEnvironment = OutputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Environment = Environment;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_end_ExitPoint
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Environment = Environment;
		IF NOT(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContextCallable,FALSE)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-ID" =  Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: SEND response to ' || Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code-Callable";
				
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_start_SetMonitoringEnvContextCallable
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment = InputLocalEnvironment.Environment;
		SET Environment.Variables."cmdb-id-source" = "cmdb-id-source" ;
		-- generate contextual id for tracking
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;				
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Global-Transaction-Id",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-Id",Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id");	
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code-Callable" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Origin-Caller-Code",COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",''));
		SET Environment.Variables."Callable-Application" = InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetApplication || ' - ' || InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetEndpointName;
			
		-- set contextual variables to track them in SUP logs		
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."callable-application" = Environment.Variables."Callable-Application";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";	
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.SetContextCallable = TRUE;	
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable request from ' || Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";		
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;
